=== file: legacy/build_os.sh
=== file build_os.sh content:
#!/bin/bash
set -e

PROJECT_NAME="mind_core"
echo ">>> Создание проекта $PROJECT_NAME..."
cargo new $PROJECT_NAME --bin
cd $PROJECT_NAME

echo ">>> Настройка Rust toolchain (nightly)..."
cat <<EOF > rust-toolchain.toml
[toolchain]
channel = "nightly"
targets = ["x86_64-unknown-uefi"]
EOF

echo ">>> Конфигурация Cargo.toml..."
cat <<EOF > Cargo.toml
[package]
name = "mind_core"
version = "0.1.0"
edition = "2021"

[dependencies]
uefi = "0.24.0"
uefi-services = "0.24.0"

[profile.release]
panic = "abort"
lto = true
EOF

echo ">>> Запись исходного кода ядра (src/main.rs)..."
cat << 'EOF' > src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use uefi::prelude::*;
use uefi::proto::console::gop::GraphicsOutput;

#[entry]
fn main(image: Handle, mut system_table: SystemTable<Boot>) -> Status {
    uefi_services::init(&mut system_table).unwrap();
    let boot_services = system_table.boot_services();

    let gop_handle = boot_services.get_handle_for_protocol::<GraphicsOutput>().unwrap();
    let mut gop = boot_services.open_protocol_exclusive::<GraphicsOutput>(gop_handle).unwrap();

    let mode_info = gop.current_mode_info();
    let (width, height) = mode_info.resolution();
    let stride = mode_info.stride();

    let mut fb = gop.frame_buffer();
    let fb_ptr = fb.as_mut_ptr();

    let center_x = width as isize / 2;
    let center_y = height as isize / 2;
    let radius: isize = 150;

    for y in 0..(height as isize) {
        for x in 0..(width as isize) {
            let dx = x - center_x;
            let dy = y - center_y;
            if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                let pixel_index = (y as usize * stride + x as usize) * 4;
                unsafe {
                    fb_ptr.add(pixel_index).write(255);     // Blue
                    fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 1).write(0);   // Green
                    fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 2).write(0);   // Red
                    fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 3).write(255); // Alpha
                }
            }
        }
    }
    loop {}
}
EOF

echo ">>> Компиляция..."
cargo build --target x86_64-unknown-uefi --release

echo ">>> Подготовка файловой структуры для флешки..."
mkdir -p usb_root/EFI/BOOT
cp target/x86_64-unknown-uefi/release/mind_core.efi usb_root/EFI/BOOT/BOOTX64.EFI

echo ""
echo "УСПЕШНО! Образ ядра находится в директории: $(pwd)/usb_root"

=== end of file build_os.sh content

=== file: legacy/build_os_02.sh
=== file build_os_02.sh content:
#!/bin/bash
set -e

PROJECT_NAME="mind_core_os"
echo ">>> [1/6] Создание структуры проекта $PROJECT_NAME..."
cargo new $PROJECT_NAME --bin
cd $PROJECT_NAME

echo ">>> [2/6] Настройка Rust toolchain..."
# Принудительно используем nightly для bare-metal разработки
cat <<EOF > rust-toolchain.toml
[toolchain]
channel = "nightly"
targets = ["x86_64-unknown-uefi"]
EOF

echo ">>> [3/6] Настройка конфигурации сборки..."
mkdir -p .cargo
cat <<EOF > .cargo/config.toml
[build]
target = "x86_64-unknown-uefi"

[target.x86_64-unknown-uefi]
rustflags = [
    "-C", "target-feature=-mmx,-sse,+soft-float",
]
EOF

echo ">>> [4/6] Формирование зависимостей (Cargo.toml)..."
cat <<EOF > Cargo.toml
[package]
name = "$PROJECT_NAME"
version = "0.2.0"
edition = "2021"

[dependencies]
uefi = "0.24.0"
uefi-services = "0.24.0"

[profile.release]
panic = "abort"
lto = true
opt-level = "z" # Оптимизация по размеру
EOF

echo ">>> [5/6] Запись исходного кода загрузчика и ядра (src/main.rs)..."
cat << 'EOF' > src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::arch::asm;
use uefi::prelude::*;
use uefi::proto::console::gop::GraphicsOutput;

// =====================================================================
// ФАЗА 2: ЯДРО ОПЕРАЦИОННОЙ СИСТЕМЫ (OS KERNEL)
// =====================================================================
// Этот код берет полный контроль над железом. В полноценной архитектуре
// он компилируется как отдельный ELF-файл, но для надежности прототипа
// слинкован вместе с загрузчиком.
fn run_os_kernel(fb_ptr: *mut u8, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize) -> ! {
    let mut radius: isize = 50;
    let mut growing = true;

    loop {
        // 1. Очистка кадра (заливка черным фоном)
        let frame_size = height * stride * 4;
        unsafe {
            core::ptr::write_bytes(fb_ptr, 0, frame_size);
        }
        
        // 2. Вычисление центра экрана
        let center_x = width as isize / 2;
        let center_y = height as isize / 2;

        // 3. Отрисовка пульсирующего круга (ядра)
        for y in 0..(height as isize) {
            for x in 0..(width as isize) {
                let dx = x - center_x;
                let dy = y - center_y;
                
                // Проверка принадлежности пикселя к радиусу круга
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let pixel_index = (y as usize * stride + x as usize) * 4;
                    unsafe {
                        // Цветовая схема: Кибер-синий (BGRA)
                        fb_ptr.add(pixel_index).write(255);     // Blue
                        fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 1).write(120); // Green
                        fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 2).write(0);   // Red
                        fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 3).write(255); // Alpha
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // 4. Математика пульсации
        if growing {
            radius += 3;
            if radius >= 250 { growing = false; }
        } else {
            radius -= 3;
            if radius <= 50 { growing = true; }
        }

        // 5. Аппаратная задержка (CPU spin-loop). 
        // Заменяет таймер, пока не написан драйвер прерываний APIC.
        for _ in 0..1_500_000 {
            unsafe { asm!("nop"); }
        }
    }
}

// =====================================================================
// ФАЗА 1: ЗАГРУЗЧИК (UEFI BOOTLOADER)
// =====================================================================
#[entry]
fn main(image: Handle, mut system_table: SystemTable<Boot>) -> Status {
    // Инициализация базовых сервисов
    uefi_services::init(&mut system_table).unwrap();
    let boot_services = system_table.boot_services();

    // Захват протокола вывода графики (Graphics Output Protocol)
    let gop_handle = boot_services.get_handle_for_protocol::<GraphicsOutput>().unwrap();
    let mut gop = boot_services.open_protocol_exclusive::<GraphicsOutput>(gop_handle).unwrap();
    
    // Получение геометрии экрана
    let mode_info = gop.current_mode_info();
    let (width, height) = mode_info.resolution();
    let stride = mode_info.stride();
    
    // Захват прямого указателя на видеопамять (фреймбуфер)
    let mut fb = gop.frame_buffer();
    let fb_ptr = fb.as_mut_ptr();

    // Передача управления Ядру ОС. 
    // На этом этапе загрузчик отдает фреймбуфер и навсегда уходит в цикл ядра.
    run_os_kernel(fb_ptr, width, height, stride);
}
EOF

echo "======================================================="

echo "======================================================="

=== file: legacy/fix_app.sh
=== file fix_app.sh content:
#!/bin/bash
set -e
source "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null || true
export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH"
ROOT_DIR=$(pwd)

echo ">>> [1/3] Исправляем приложение (app/src/main.rs)..."

# Создаем исправленный файл приложения целиком, чтобы избежать ошибок седирования
cat << 'INNER_APP' > app/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::panic::PanicInfo;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SystemApi {
    pub draw_string_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const u8, usize, u32),
    pub get_ticks_fn: extern "sysv64" fn() -> usize,
    pub read_file_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*const u8, usize, *mut u8, usize) -> usize,
}

const SIN_TABLE: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];

fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 {
        buf[0] = b'0';
        return 1;
    }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx {
        buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i];
    }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, api_ptr: *const SystemApi) -> ! {
    let api = unsafe { &*api_ptr };
    
    let mut file_buf = [0u8; 64];
    let filename = b"welcome.txt";
    let bytes_read = (api.read_file_fn)(filename.as_ptr(), filename.len(), file_buf.as_mut_ptr(), file_buf.len());
    let file_str = if bytes_read > 0 { &file_buf[..bytes_read] } else { b"FILE NOT FOUND" };

    let mut frame: usize = 0;
    let mut cycle_counter: usize = 0;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    loop {
        cycle_counter += 1;

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 120;
        let mut t_temp = frame / 8; 
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = SIN_TABLE[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = SIN_TABLE[cos_angle];

        for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
            for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), 0xFF00FF00); }
                }
            }
        }

        // Исправленные вызовы с передачей .as_ptr()
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, b"USERSPACE APP RUNNING".as_ptr(), 21, 0x0000FF00);
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, file_str.as_ptr(), file_str.len(), 0x0000FFFF);

        let mut num_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let len = usize_to_str(cycle_counter, &mut num_buf);
        
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 100, b"CYCLES:".as_ptr(), 7, 0x00FFFFFF);
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 100, num_buf.as_ptr(), len, 0x00FFFF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        
        frame += 1;
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_APP

echo ">>> [2/3] Пересборка компонентов..."

echo "=========================================================="

=== file: legacy/fix_app_counter.sh
=== file fix_app_counter.sh content:
#!/bin/bash
set -e
source "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null || true
export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH"
ROOT_DIR=$(pwd)

echo ">>> Обновляем Приложение (Квадрат + RDTSC Счетчик + Полный Шрифт)..."
cat << 'INNER_APP' > app/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::panic::PanicInfo;
use core::arch::asm;

// Чтение аппаратного счетчика тактов процессора (Time-Stamp Counter)
fn get_rdtsc() -> u64 {
    let lo: u32;
    let hi: u32;
    unsafe {
        asm!("rdtsc", out("eax") lo, out("edx") hi, options(nomem, nostack));
    }
    ((hi as u64) << 32) | (lo as u64)
}

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            // Восстановлен ПОЛНЫЙ алфавит от A до Z и цифры
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0' => (row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7,
                b'1' => col == 4 || (row == 1 && col == 3) || row == 7,
                b'2' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 3 && row < 7) || row == 7,
                b'3' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 7 && row != 3,
                b'4' => col == 6 || (row == 4) || (col == 1 && row < 4) || (row + col == 6 && col < 6),
                b'5' => row == 0 || (col == 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7),
                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => (row == 2 || row == 5) && (col == 3 || col == 4), // Двоеточие исправлено (теперь точки, а не полосы)
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

// Теперь конвертируем 64-битный счетчик (до 18 квинтиллионов)
fn u64_to_str(mut val: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, _app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    let sin_table: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];
    
    let mut frame: usize = 0;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    loop {
        // Очистка буфера
        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 120;
        let mut t_temp = frame / 2; // Восстановили нормальное вращение
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = sin_table[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = sin_table[cos_angle];

        // Отрисовка квадрата
        for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
            for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), 0xFF00FF00); }
                }
            }
        }

        let app_msg = [b'U', b'S', b'E', b'R', b'S', b'P', b'A', b'C', b'E'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, &app_msg, 0x0000FF00);

        // Читаем хардварные циклы (будет меняться на миллионы каждую долю секунды!)
        let cycles = get_rdtsc();
        
        let mut num_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let len = u64_to_str(cycles, &mut num_buf);
        let cyc_msg = [b'C', b'Y', b'C', b'L', b'E', b'S', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, &cyc_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 70, &num_buf[0..len], 0x00FFFF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        
        frame += 1;
        // Никаких искусственных тормозов.
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }

#!/bin/bash
set -e
source "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null || true
export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH"
ROOT_DIR=$(pwd)

cat << 'INNER_APP' > app/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::panic::PanicInfo;

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0' => (row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7,
                b'1' => col == 4 || (row == 1 && col == 3) || row == 7,
                b'2' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 3 && row < 7) || row == 7,
                b'3' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 7 && row != 3,
                b'4' => col == 6 || (row == 4) || (col == 1 && row < 4) || (row + col == 6 && col < 6),
                b'5' => row == 0 || (col == 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7),
                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => (row == 2 || row == 5) && (col == 3 || col == 4),
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, _app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    let sin_table: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];
    
    let mut frame_counter: usize = 0;
    let mut work_counter: usize = 0;
    
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    loop {
        // Параллельный рабочий цикл
        // Volatile чтение предотвращает удаление цикла оптимизатором компилятора
        for _ in 0..1000 {
            work_counter += 1;
            unsafe { core::ptr::read_volatile(&work_counter); }
        }

        frame_counter += 1;

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 120;
        
        // Делитель увеличен с 5 до 20 для значительного замедления вращения квадрата
        let mut t_temp = frame_counter / 20; 
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = sin_table[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = sin_table[cos_angle];

        for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
            for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), 0xFF00FF00); }
                }
            }
        }

        let app_msg = [b'U', b'S', b'E', b'R', b'S', b'P', b'A', b'C', b'E'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, &app_msg, 0x0000FF00);

        // Вывод счетчика рендеринга (кадры)
        let mut f_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let f_len = usize_to_str(frame_counter, &mut f_buf);
        let f_msg = [b'F', b'R', b'A', b'M', b'E', b'S', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, &f_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 70, &f_buf[0..f_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        // Вывод счетчика параллельного рабочего цикла
        let mut w_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let w_len = usize_to_str(work_counter, &mut w_buf);
        let w_msg = [b'W', b'O', b'R', b'K', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 100, &w_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 100, &w_buf[0..w_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_APP

cd "$ROOT_DIR/app" && cargo build --release
OBJCOPY=$(find $(rustc --print sysroot) -name llvm-objcopy | head -n 1)
$OBJCOPY -O binary -j .text -j .rodata -j .data "$ROOT_DIR/app/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/app" "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/src/app.bin"

echo "DONE"

#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::panic::PanicInfo;

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'0' => (row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7,
                b'1' => col == 4 || (row == 1 && col == 3) || row == 7,
                b'2' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 3 && row < 7) || row == 7,
                b'3' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 7 && row != 3,
                b'4' => col == 6 || (row == 4) || (col == 1 && row < 4) || (row + col == 6 && col < 6),
                b'5' => row == 0 || (col == 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7),
                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => row == 2 || row == 5,
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {

    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, _app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    let sin_table: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];
    
    let mut frame: usize = 0;
    let mut cycle_counter: usize = 0;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    loop {
        cycle_counter += 1;

        // Очистка заднего буфера
        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 120;
        // Сильно увеличенный делитель, так как теперь цикл выполняется без задержек (nop)
        let mut t_temp = frame / 500; 
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = sin_table[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = sin_table[cos_angle];

        // Отрисовка квадрата
        for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
            for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), 0xFF00FF00); }
                }
            }
        }

        let app_msg = [b'U', b'S', b'E', b'R', b'S', b'P', b'A', b'C', b'E'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, &app_msg, 0x0000FF00);

        let mut num_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let len = usize_to_str(cycle_counter, &mut num_buf);
        let cyc_msg = [b'C', b'Y', b'C', b'L', b'E', b'S', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, &cyc_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 70, &num_buf[0..len], 0x00FFFF00);

        // Копирование фреймбуфера (это и есть главное "узкое горлышко" скорости)
        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        
        frame += 1;
        // Никаких nop. Цикл работает на пределе возможностей "железа".
    }
}

=== file: legacy/fix_final.sh
=== file fix_final.sh content:
#!/bin/bash
set -e
source "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null || true
export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH"
ROOT_DIR=$(pwd)

echo ">>> [1/3] ЯДРО: Восстановление стабильного рендера и RAMFS..."
cat << 'INNER_KERNEL' > kernel/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SystemApi {
    pub draw_string_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const u8, usize, u32),
    pub get_ticks_fn: extern "sysv64" fn() -> usize,
    pub read_file_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*const u8, usize, *mut u8, usize) -> usize,
}

extern "sysv64" fn sys_draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text_ptr: *const u8, text_len: usize, color: u32) {
    if text_ptr.is_null() { return; }
    let text = unsafe { core::slice::from_raw_parts(text_ptr, text_len) };
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        if ascii != b' ' { draw_char_internal(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color); }
        cx += 10;
    }
}

static mut SYSTEM_TICKS: usize = 0;
extern "sysv64" fn sys_get_ticks() -> usize { unsafe { SYSTEM_TICKS } }

// Функция сравнения строк без зависимости от memcmp
fn strings_equal(a: &[u8], b: &[u8]) -> bool {
    if a.len() != b.len() { return false; }
    for i in 0..a.len() { if a[i] != b[i] { return false; } }
    true
}

extern "sysv64" fn sys_read_file(name_ptr: *const u8, name_len: usize, buf: *mut u8, buf_len: usize) -> usize {
    if name_ptr.is_null() || buf.is_null() { return 0; }
    let name = unsafe { core::slice::from_raw_parts(name_ptr, name_len) };
    
    // Безопасный RAMFS (строки генерируются относительно регистра RIP, без абсолютных адресов)
    if strings_equal(name, b"welcome.txt") {
        let content = b"MIND CORE OS: RAMFS READ SUCCESS!";
        let copy_len = if content.len() < buf_len { content.len() } else { buf_len };
        unsafe { core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(content.as_ptr(), buf, copy_len); }
        return copy_len;
    }
    0
}

fn draw_char_internal(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0'..=b'9' => row == 0 || row == 7 || col == 0 || col == 7,
                b':' => row == 2 || row == 5,
                b'-' => row == 3,
                b'.' => row >= 6 && col >= 3 && col <= 4,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    unsafe { asm!("cli"); }
    let total_pixels = height * stride;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1; 
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let w_isize = width as isize;
    let h_isize = height as isize;

    let mut time: usize = 0;
    let mut wave: usize = 0;
    let timeout_frames = 150; // Быстрый переход для теста

    // Инициализация API на стеке - безопасно для UEFI
    let api = SystemApi {
        draw_string_fn: sys_draw_string,
        get_ticks_fn: sys_get_ticks,
        read_file_fn: sys_read_file,
    };

    loop {
        unsafe { SYSTEM_TICKS = time; }
        let mut status: u8;
        let mut scancode: u8 = 0;
        unsafe {
            asm!("in al, 0x64", out("al") status);
            if (status & 1) == 1 { asm!("in al, 0x60", out("al") scancode); }
        }

        if time > timeout_frames || (scancode != 0 && scancode < 0x80) {
            let app_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, *mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const SystemApi) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(app_ptr) };
            
            // Защита от потери API-структуры
            app_entry(fb_ptr, bb_ptr, width, height, stride, &api);
            unsafe { asm!("nop"); } 
            loop {} 
        }

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00000000); } }

        let phase = (wave / 3) % 100;
        let pulse = if phase < 50 { phase as isize } else { (100 - phase) as isize };
        let radius = 100 + (pulse / 2);
        
        for y in 0..h_isize {
            for x in 0..w_isize {
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let offset = (y as usize) * stride + (x as usize);
                    let color = 0xFF000000 | (((pulse * 3) as u32) << 16) | (140 << 8) | 255;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), color); }
                }
            }
        }

        let msg_os = b"MIND CORE OS";
        let msg_zen = b"ZEN SPHERE";
        sys_draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 50, msg_os.as_ptr(), msg_os.len(), 0x00FFFFFF);
        sys_draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 80, msg_zen.as_ptr(), msg_zen.len(), 0x0000FF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        wave += 1;
        time += 1;
        for _ in 0..2_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_KERNEL

echo ">>> [2/3] ПРИЛОЖЕНИЕ: Очищенный код с плавным квадратом..."
cat << 'INNER_APP' > app/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::panic::PanicInfo;
use core::arch::asm;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SystemApi {
    pub draw_string_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const u8, usize, u32),
    pub get_ticks_fn: extern "sysv64" fn() -> usize,
    pub read_file_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*const u8, usize, *mut u8, usize) -> usize,
}

const SIN_TABLE: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];

fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, api_ptr: *const SystemApi) -> ! {
    let api = unsafe { &*api_ptr };
    
    let mut file_buf = [0u8; 64];
    let fname = b"welcome.txt";
    let bytes_read = (api.read_file_fn)(fname.as_ptr(), fname.len(), file_buf.as_mut_ptr(), file_buf.len());
    let file_str = if bytes_read > 0 { &file_buf[..bytes_read] } else { b"FILE NOT FOUND" };

    let mut frame: usize = 0;
    let mut cycle_counter: usize = 0;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    loop {
        cycle_counter += 1;

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 120;
        let mut t_temp = frame / 10; 
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = SIN_TABLE[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = SIN_TABLE[cos_angle];

        for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
            for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), 0xFF00FF00); }
                }
            }
        }

        let app_msg = b"USERSPACE APP RUNNING";
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, app_msg.as_ptr(), app_msg.len(), 0x0000FF00);
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, file_str.as_ptr(), file_str.len(), 0x0000FFFF);

        let mut num_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let len = usize_to_str(cycle_counter, &mut num_buf);
        let cyc_msg = b"CYCLES:";
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 100, cyc_msg.as_ptr(), cyc_msg.len(), 0x00FFFFFF);
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 100, num_buf.as_ptr(), len, 0x00FFFF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        
        frame += 1;
        // Задержка возвращена, чтобы квадрат не превращался в размытое пятно
        for _ in 0..500_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_APP

cd "$ROOT_DIR/app" && cargo build --release

$OBJCOPY -O binary -j .text -j .rodata -j .data "$ROOT_DIR/kernel/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/kernel" "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/src/kernel.bin"
$OBJCOPY -O binary -j .text -j .rodata -j .data "$ROOT_DIR/app/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/app" "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/src/app.bin"

cd "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader" && cargo build --release --target x86_64-unknown-uefi
cp "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/target/x86_64-unknown-uefi/release/bootloader.efi" "$ROOT_DIR/usb_root/EFI/BOOT/BOOTX64.EFI"

echo "=========================================================="

=== file: legacy/fix_handoff.sh
=== file fix_handoff.sh content:
#!/bin/bash
set -e
source "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null || true
export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH"
ROOT_DIR=$(pwd)

echo ">>> Исправляем Ядро (упрощаем стабильный переход)..."
cat << 'INNER_KERNEL' > kernel/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SystemApi {
    pub draw_string_fn: usize,
    pub read_file_fn: usize,
}

struct VirtualFile {
    name: &'static [u8],
    content: &'static [u8],
}

const RAMFS: &[VirtualFile] = &[
    VirtualFile {
        name: b"welcome.txt",
        content: b"MIND CORE OS v0.9 - RAMFS ACTIVE",
    },
];

extern "sysv64" fn sys_draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text_ptr: *const u8, text_len: usize, color: u32) {
    if text_ptr.is_null() { return; }
    let text = unsafe { core::slice::from_raw_parts(text_ptr, text_len) };
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        if ascii != b' ' {
            draw_char_internal(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        }
        cx += 10;
    }
}

extern "sysv64" fn sys_read_file(name_ptr: *const u8, name_len: usize, buf: *mut u8, buf_len: usize) -> usize {
    if name_ptr.is_null() || buf.is_null() { return 0; }
    let name = unsafe { core::slice::from_raw_parts(name_ptr, name_len) };
    for file in RAMFS {
        if file.name == name {
            let copy_len = if file.content.len() < buf_len { file.content.len() } else { buf_len };
            unsafe { core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(file.content.as_ptr(), buf, copy_len); }
            return copy_len;
        }
    }
    0
}

fn draw_char_internal(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0'..=b'9' => row == 0 || row == 7 || col == 0 || col == 7,
                b':' => row == 2 || row == 5,
                b'-' => row == 3,
                b'.' => row >= 6 && col >= 3 && col <= 4,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    unsafe { asm!("cli"); }
    let total_pixels = height * stride;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1; 
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let w_isize = width as isize;
    let h_isize = height as isize;

    let mut time: usize = 0;
    let mut wave: usize = 0;
    let timeout_frames = 150; // Быстрый переход для теста (~3-4 сек)

    let api = SystemApi {
        draw_string_fn: sys_draw_string as usize,
        read_file_fn: sys_read_file as usize,
    };

    loop {
        let mut status: u8;
        let mut scancode: u8 = 0;
        unsafe {
            asm!("in al, 0x64", out("al") status);
            if (status & 1) == 1 { asm!("in al, 0x60", out("al") scancode); }
        }

        if time > timeout_frames || (scancode != 0 && scancode < 0x80) {
            // Белая вспышка перехода
            for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00FFFFFF); } }
            for _ in 0..5_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
            
            let app_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, *mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const SystemApi) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(app_ptr) };
            app_entry(fb_ptr, bb_ptr, width, height, stride, &api as *const SystemApi);
        }

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00000000); } }

        let phase = (wave / 3) % 100;
        let pulse = if phase < 50 { phase as isize } else { (100 - phase) as isize };
        let radius = 100 + (pulse / 2);
        
        for y in 0..h_isize {
            for x in 0..w_isize {
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let offset = (y as usize) * stride + (x as usize);
                    let color = 0xFF000000 | (((pulse * 3) as u32) << 16) | (140 << 8) | 255;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), color); }
                }
            }
        }

        sys_draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 50, b"MIND CORE OS".as_ptr(), 12, 0x00FFFFFF);
        sys_draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 80, b"ZEN SPHERE".as_ptr(), 10, 0x0000FF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        wave += 1;
        time += 1;
        for _ in 0..2_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }

    "-C", "link-arg=-Tlinker.ld",

CONFIG

sed -i 's/sys_draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 50, b"MIND CORE OS", 12, 0x00FFFFFF);/sys_draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 50, b"MIND CORE OS".as_ptr(), 12, 0x00FFFFFF);/g' kernel/src/main.rs
sed -i 's/sys_draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 80, b"ZEN SPHERE", 10, 0x0000FF00);/sys_draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 80, b"ZEN SPHERE".as_ptr(), 10, 0x0000FF00);/g' kernel/src/main.rs

#!/bin/bash

ROOT_DIR=$(pwd)

echo ">>> [1/3] ЯДРО: Избавляемся от статики, собираем строки на стеке..."
cat << 'INNER_KERNEL' > kernel/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SystemApi {
    pub draw_string_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const u8, usize, u32),
    pub get_ticks_fn: extern "sysv64" fn() -> usize,
    pub read_file_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*const u8, usize, *mut u8, usize) -> usize,
}

extern "sysv64" fn sys_draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text_ptr: *const u8, text_len: usize, color: u32) {
    if text_ptr.is_null() { return; }
    let text = unsafe { core::slice::from_raw_parts(text_ptr, text_len) };
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        if ascii != b' ' { draw_char_internal(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color); }
        cx += 10;
    }
}

extern "sysv64" fn sys_get_ticks() -> usize { 0 }

extern "sysv64" fn sys_read_file(name_ptr: *const u8, name_len: usize, buf: *mut u8, buf_len: usize) -> usize {
    if name_ptr.is_null() || buf.is_null() { return 0; }
    let name = unsafe { core::slice::from_raw_parts(name_ptr, name_len) };
    
    // RAMFS без абсолютных адресов (формируем на стеке)
    let fname = [b'w', b'e', b'l', b'c', b'o', b'm', b'e', b'.', b't', b'x', b't'];
    
    if name.len() == fname.len() {
        let mut match_ok = true;
        for i in 0..fname.len() { if name[i] != fname[i] { match_ok = false; break; } }
        
        if match_ok {
            let content = [b'A', b'P', b'I', b' ', b'R', b'E', b'A', b'D', b'Y', b' ', b'-', b' ', b'R', b'A', b'M', b'F', b'S', b' ', b'O', b'K'];
            let copy_len = if content.len() < buf_len { content.len() } else { buf_len };
            unsafe { core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(content.as_ptr(), buf, copy_len); }
            return copy_len;
        }
    }
    0
}

                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),

                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,

                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    unsafe { asm!("cli"); }
    let total_pixels = height * stride;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1; 
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let w_isize = width as isize;
    let h_isize = height as isize;

    let mut time: usize = 0;
    let mut wave: usize = 0;
    let timeout_frames = 200;

    // API на стеке - самое безопасное место!
    let api = SystemApi {
        draw_string_fn: sys_draw_string,
        get_ticks_fn: sys_get_ticks,
        read_file_fn: sys_read_file,
    };

    loop {
        let mut status: u8;
        let mut scancode: u8 = 0;
        unsafe {
            asm!("in al, 0x64", out("al") status);
            if (status & 1) == 1 { asm!("in al, 0x60", out("al") scancode); }
        }

        if time > timeout_frames || (scancode != 0 && scancode < 0x80) {
            let app_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, *mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const SystemApi) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(app_ptr) };
            
            // Защита от потери локальных переменных!
            app_entry(fb_ptr, bb_ptr, width, height, stride, &api);
            unsafe { asm!("nop"); } // Анти-Tail Call
            loop {} // Вечная петля гарант того, что стек ядра жив
        }

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00000000); } }

        let phase = (wave / 3) % 100;
        let pulse = if phase < 50 { phase as isize } else { (100 - phase) as isize };
        let radius = 100 + (pulse / 2);
        
        for y in 0..h_isize {
            for x in 0..w_isize {
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let offset = (y as usize) * stride + (x as usize);
                    let color = 0xFF000000 | (((pulse * 3) as u32) << 16) | (140 << 8) | 255;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), color); }
                }
            }
        }

        // --- ЦВЕТОВАЯ ТРАССИРОВКА: Вспышка красным перед опасной зоной ---
        if time == 0 {
            for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), 0x00FF0000); } }
            for _ in 0..10_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
        }

        // Строки на стеке (100% защита от абсолютных адресов)
        let t1 = [b'M', b'I', b'N', b'D', b' ', b'C', b'O', b'R', b'E'];
        let t2 = [b'Z', b'E', b'N', b' ', b'S', b'P', b'H', b'E', b'R', b'E'];
        sys_draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 50, t1.as_ptr(), t1.len(), 0x00FFFFFF);
        sys_draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 80, t2.as_ptr(), t2.len(), 0x0000FF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        wave += 1;
        time += 1;
        for _ in 0..2_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_KERNEL

cat << 'INNER_APP' > app/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::panic::PanicInfo;
use core::arch::asm;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SystemApi {
    pub draw_string_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const u8, usize, u32),
    pub get_ticks_fn: extern "sysv64" fn() -> usize,
    pub read_file_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*const u8, usize, *mut u8, usize) -> usize,
}

fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, api_ptr: *const SystemApi) -> ! {
    let api = unsafe { &*api_ptr };
    
    // Массивы и таблицы изолированы внутри функции на стеке
    let sin_table: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];
    
    let mut file_buf = [0u8; 64];
    let fname = [b'w', b'e', b'l', b'c', b'o', b'm', b'e', b'.', b't', b'x', b't'];
    let bytes_read = (api.read_file_fn)(fname.as_ptr(), fname.len(), file_buf.as_mut_ptr(), file_buf.len());
    let file_str = if bytes_read > 0 { &file_buf[..bytes_read] } else { b"FILE NOT FOUND".as_ref() };

    let mut frame: usize = 0;
    let mut cycle_counter: usize = 0;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    loop {
        cycle_counter += 1;

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 120;
        let mut t_temp = frame / 10; 
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = sin_table[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = sin_table[cos_angle];

        for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
            for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), 0xFF00FF00); }
                }
            }
        }

        let app_msg = [b'U', b'S', b'E', b'R', b'S', b'P', b'A', b'C', b'E', b' ', b'A', b'C', b'T', b'I', b'V', b'E'];
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, app_msg.as_ptr(), app_msg.len(), 0x0000FF00);
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, file_str.as_ptr(), file_str.len(), 0x0000FFFF);

        let mut num_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let len = usize_to_str(cycle_counter, &mut num_buf);
        let cyc_msg = [b'C', b'Y', b'C', b'L', b'E', b'S', b':'];
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 100, cyc_msg.as_ptr(), cyc_msg.len(), 0x00FFFFFF);
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 100, num_buf.as_ptr(), len, 0x00FFFF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        frame += 1;
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_APP

#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0'..=b'9' => row == 0 || row == 7 || col == 0 || col == 7, // Упрощенные цифры
                b':' => row == 2 || row == 5,
                b'-' => row == 3,
                b'.' => row >= 6 && col >= 3 && col <= 4,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        if ascii != b' ' { draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color); }
        cx += 10;
    }
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    unsafe { asm!("cli"); }
    let total_pixels = height * stride;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1; 
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let w_isize = width as isize;
    let h_isize = height as isize;

    let mut time: usize = 0;
    let mut wave: usize = 0;
    let timeout_frames = 600; // Честные 15 секунд дзена

    loop {
        let mut status: u8;
        let mut scancode: u8 = 0;
        unsafe {
            asm!("in al, 0x64", out("al") status);
            if (status & 1) == 1 { asm!("in al, 0x60", out("al") scancode); }
        }

        if time > timeout_frames || (scancode != 0 && scancode < 0x80) {
            for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00FFFFFF); } }
            for _ in 0..10_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
            
            // ПРОСТОЙ БЕЗОПАСНЫЙ ПРЫЖОК
            let app_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, *mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const u8) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(app_ptr) };
            app_entry(fb_ptr, bb_ptr, width, height, stride, core::ptr::null());
        }

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00000000); } }

        let phase = (wave / 3) % 100;
        let pulse = if phase < 50 { phase as isize } else { (100 - phase) as isize };
        let radius = 100 + (pulse / 2);
        
        for y in 0..h_isize {
            for x in 0..w_isize {
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let offset = (y as usize) * stride + (x as usize);
                    let color = 0xFF000000 | (((pulse * 3) as u32) << 16) | (140 << 8) | 255;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), color); }
                }
            }
        }

        // Текст собирается прямо на стеке, не крашит память!
        let msg_core = [b'M', b'I', b'N', b'D', b' ', b'C', b'O', b'R', b'E', b' ', b'O', b'S'];
        let msg_zen = [b'Z', b'E', b'N', b' ', b'S', b'P', b'H', b'E', b'R', b'E'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 50, &msg_core, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 80, &msg_zen, 0x0000FF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        wave += 1;
        time += 1;
        for _ in 0..4_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }

#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::panic::PanicInfo;
use core::arch::asm;

// Та же самая функция отрисовки, скопированная внутрь приложения
fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'0' => (row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7,
                b'1' => col == 4 || (row == 1 && col == 3) || row == 7,
                b'2' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 3 && row < 7) || row == 7,
                b'3' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 7 && row != 3,
                b'4' => col == 6 || (row == 4) || (col == 1 && row < 4) || (row + col == 6 && col < 6),
                b'5' => row == 0 || (col == 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7),
                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => row == 2 || row == 5,
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, _app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    let sin_table: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];
    
    let mut frame: usize = 0;
    let mut cycle_counter: usize = 0;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    loop {
        cycle_counter += 1;

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 120;
        let mut t_temp = frame / 10; // Скорость вращения
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = sin_table[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = sin_table[cos_angle];

        for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
            for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), 0xFF00FF00); }
                }
            }
        }

        // Вывод заголовка
        let app_msg = [b'U', b'S', b'E', b'R', b'S', b'P', b'A', b'C', b'E'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, &app_msg, 0x0000FF00);

        // ВЫВОД СЧЕТЧИКА ЦИКЛОВ
        let mut num_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let len = usize_to_str(cycle_counter, &mut num_buf);
        let cyc_msg = [b'C', b'Y', b'C', b'L', b'E', b'S', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, &cyc_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 70, &num_buf[0..len], 0x00FFFF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        
        frame += 1;
        // Задержка нужна, иначе счетчик будет крутиться миллионы раз в секунду и станет размытым пятном
        for _ in 0..500_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_APP

echo ">>> [3/3] Чистая Сборка..."

cd "$ROOT_DIR/app" && cargo build --release

# Собираем с .rodata, чтобы switch/match работал корректно
$OBJCOPY -O binary -j .text -j .rodata -j .data "$ROOT_DIR/kernel/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/kernel" "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/src/kernel.bin"
$OBJCOPY -O binary -j .text -j .rodata -j .data "$ROOT_DIR/app/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/app" "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/src/app.bin"

cd "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader" && cargo build --release --target x86_64-unknown-uefi
