=== file: legacy/fix_software_counter.sh
=== file fix_software_counter.sh content:
#!/bin/bash
set -e
source "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null || true
export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH"
ROOT_DIR=$(pwd)

echo ">>> Обновляем Приложение (Настоящий программный счетчик итераций)..."
cat << 'INNER_APP' > app/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::panic::PanicInfo;
use core::arch::asm;

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0' => (row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7,
                b'1' => col == 4 || (row == 1 && col == 3) || row == 7,
                b'2' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 3 && row < 7) || row == 7,
                b'3' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 7 && row != 3,
                b'4' => col == 6 || (row == 4) || (col == 1 && row < 4) || (row + col == 6 && col < 6),
                b'5' => row == 0 || (col == 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7),
                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => (row == 2 || row == 5) && (col == 3 || col == 4),
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, _app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    let sin_table: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];
    
    let mut frame: usize = 0;
    // НАСТОЯЩИЙ ПРОГРАММНЫЙ СЧЕТЧИК
    let mut cycle_counter: usize = 0; 
    
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    loop {
        // +1 на каждую итерацию главного цикла программы
        cycle_counter += 1;

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 120;
        let mut t_temp = frame / 5; // Вращаем квадрат 
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = sin_table[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = sin_table[cos_angle];

        for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
            for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), 0xFF00FF00); }
                }
            }
        }

        let app_msg = [b'U', b'S', b'E', b'R', b'S', b'P', b'A', b'C', b'E'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, &app_msg, 0x0000FF00);

        // Выводим программный счетчик циклов на экран
        let mut num_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let len = usize_to_str(cycle_counter, &mut num_buf);
        let cyc_msg = [b'C', b'Y', b'C', b'L', b'E', b'S', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, &cyc_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 70, &num_buf[0..len], 0x00FFFF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        
        frame += 1;
        // Задержек нет. Цикл крутится на своей естественной максимальной скорости.
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_APP

echo ">>> Сборка Приложения и Загрузчика..."
cd "$ROOT_DIR/app" && cargo build --release

OBJCOPY=$(find $(rustc --print sysroot) -name llvm-objcopy | head -n 1)
$OBJCOPY -O binary -j .text -j .rodata -j .data "$ROOT_DIR/app/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/app" "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/src/app.bin"

cd "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader" && cargo build --release --target x86_64-unknown-uefi
cp "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/target/x86_64-unknown-uefi/release/bootloader.efi" "$ROOT_DIR/usb_root/EFI/BOOT/BOOTX64.EFI"

echo "Готово! Установлен чистый программный счетчик итераций."

#!/bin/bash

PROJECT_DIR="gc_undocumented_func"

mkdir -p $PROJECT_DIR/{bootloader,kernel,app}/src

cat << 'EOF' > $PROJECT_DIR/kernel/.cargo/config.toml

cat << 'EOF' > $PROJECT_DIR/kernel/linker.ld
ENTRY(_start)
SECTIONS {
    . = 0x0;
    .text : {
        *(.text._start)
        *(.text .text.*)
    }
    .rodata : { *(.rodata .rodata.*) }
    .data : { *(.data .data.*) }
    .bss : { *(.bss .bss.*) }
    /DISCARD/ : { *(.eh_frame) }
}
EOF

cat << 'EOF' > $PROJECT_DIR/kernel/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct BootInfo {
    pub fb_ptr: *mut u8,
    pub width: usize,
    pub height: usize,
    pub stride: usize,
    pub app_ptr: *const u8,
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(info: &BootInfo) -> ! {
    let mut time: usize = 0;
    loop {
        let mut status: u8;
        let mut scancode: u8 = 0;
        unsafe {
            asm!("in al, 0x64", out("al") status);
            if (status & 1) == 1 { asm!("in al, 0x60", out("al") scancode); }
        }
        
        // 0x39 - сканкод Пробела
        if scancode == 0x39 {
            let app_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(&BootInfo) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(info.app_ptr) };
            app_entry(info);
        }

        let wave = (time % 100) as isize;
        let pulse = if wave < 50 { wave } else { 100 - wave };
        let radius = 100 + pulse;
        
        unsafe { core::ptr::write_bytes(info.fb_ptr, 0, info.height * info.stride * 4); }

        let cx = (info.width as isize) / 2;
        let cy = (info.height as isize) / 2;

        for y in 0..(info.height as isize) {
            for x in 0..(info.width as isize) {
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let pixel_index = (y as usize * info.stride + x as usize) * 4;
                    unsafe {
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index).write(255);       // B
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 1).write(100);   // G
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 2).write(pulse as u8 * 2); // R
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 3).write(255);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        time += 1;
        for _ in 0..1_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
EOF

#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct BootInfo {
    pub fb_ptr: *mut u8,
    pub width: usize,
    pub height: usize,
    pub stride: usize,
    pub app_ptr: *const u8,
}

const SIN_TABLE: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(info: &BootInfo) -> ! {
    let mut time: usize = 0;
    loop {
        unsafe { core::ptr::write_bytes(info.fb_ptr, 0, info.height * info.stride * 4); }
        let cx = (info.width as isize) / 2;
        let cy = (info.height as isize) / 2;
        let size: isize = 150;

        let angle = time % 36;
        let sin_a = SIN_TABLE[angle];
        let cos_a = SIN_TABLE[(angle + 9) % 36];

        for y in (cy - 200)..(cy + 200) {
            for x in (cx - 200)..(cx + 200) {
                if x < 0 || x >= info.width as isize || y < 0 || y >= info.height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let pixel_index = (y as usize * info.stride + x as usize) * 4;
                    unsafe {
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index).write(0);
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 1).write(255);
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 2).write(0);
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 3).write(255);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        time += 1;
        for _ in 0..1_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
EOF

[package]

[profile.release]

cat << 'EOF' > $PROJECT_DIR/bootloader/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::panic::PanicInfo;
use uefi::prelude::*;
use uefi::proto::console::gop::GraphicsOutput;
use uefi::table::boot::{AllocateType, MemoryType};

#[repr(C)]
pub struct BootInfo {
    pub fb_ptr: *mut u8,
    pub width: usize,
    pub height: usize,
    pub stride: usize,
    pub app_ptr: *const u8,
}

#[entry]
fn main(_image: Handle, mut system_table: SystemTable<Boot>) -> Status {
    let boot_services = system_table.boot_services();

    let kernel_bytes = include_bytes!("kernel.bin");
    let app_bytes = include_bytes!("app.bin");

    let kernel_pages = (kernel_bytes.len() / 4096) + 1;
    let kernel_addr = boot_services.allocate_pages(AllocateType::AnyPages, MemoryType::LOADER_DATA, kernel_pages).unwrap();
    unsafe { core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(kernel_bytes.as_ptr(), kernel_addr as *mut u8, kernel_bytes.len()); }

    let app_pages = (app_bytes.len() / 4096) + 1;
    let app_addr = boot_services.allocate_pages(AllocateType::AnyPages, MemoryType::LOADER_DATA, app_pages).unwrap();
    unsafe { core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(app_bytes.as_ptr(), app_addr as *mut u8, app_bytes.len()); }

    let gop_handle = boot_services.get_handle_for_protocol::<GraphicsOutput>().unwrap();
    let mut gop = boot_services.open_protocol_exclusive::<GraphicsOutput>(gop_handle).unwrap();
    let mode = gop.current_mode_info();
    
    let boot_info = BootInfo {
        fb_ptr: gop.frame_buffer().as_mut_ptr(),
        width: mode.resolution().0,
        height: mode.resolution().1,
        stride: mode.stride(),
        app_ptr: app_addr as *const u8,
    };

    // Исправление: передача MemoryType для uefi 0.27.0
    let (_system_table, _memory_map) = system_table.exit_boot_services(MemoryType::LOADER_DATA);
    let kernel_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(&BootInfo) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(kernel_addr) };
    kernel_entry(&boot_info);
}

// Исправление: ручной обработчик паники
#[panic_handler]
fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! {
    loop {}
}
EOF

#!/bin/bash

echo ">>> Проверка окружения..."
if ! command -v rustup &> /dev/null; then
    echo "Rust не найден. Запускаю установку..."
    curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -sSf https://sh.rustup.rs | sh -s -- -y
    source "$HOME/.cargo/env"
else
    echo "Rust установлен, обновляем..."
    rustup update
fi

echo ">>> Настройка Toolchain (Nightly) и таргетов..."
rustup default nightly
rustup target add x86_64-unknown-uefi x86_64-unknown-none
rustup component add llvm-tools-preview

echo ">>> Окружение готово к сборке!"
EOF

pause

#!/bin/bash

# Это генератор. Он ничего не устанавливает и не компилирует.
# Только создает структуру проекта и нужные файлы.

[profile.release]

opt-level = 3

cat << 'EOF' > $PROJECT_DIR/kernel/.cargo/config.toml
[build]
target = "x86_64-unknown-none"
rustflags = ["-C", "link-arg=-Tlinker.ld", "-C", "relocation-model=pic"]
EOF

cat << 'EOF' > $PROJECT_DIR/kernel/linker.ld
ENTRY(_start)
SECTIONS {
    . = 0x0;
    .text : {
        *(.text._start)
        *(.text .text.*)
    }
    .rodata : { *(.rodata .rodata.*) }
    .data : { *(.data .data.*) }
    .bss : { *(.bss .bss.*) }
    /DISCARD/ : { *(.eh_frame) }
}
EOF

cat << 'EOF' > $PROJECT_DIR/kernel/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct BootInfo {
    pub fb_ptr: *mut u8,
    pub width: usize,
    pub height: usize,
    pub stride: usize,
    pub app_ptr: *const u8,
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(info: &BootInfo) -> ! {
    let mut time: usize = 0;
    loop {
        let mut status: u8;
        let mut scancode: u8 = 0;
        unsafe {
            asm!("in al, 0x64", out("al") status);
            if (status & 1) == 1 { asm!("in al, 0x60", out("al") scancode); }
        }
        
        // 0x39 - сканкод Пробела
        if scancode == 0x39 {
            let app_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(&BootInfo) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(info.app_ptr) };
            app_entry(info);
        }

        let wave = (time % 100) as isize;
        let pulse = if wave < 50 { wave } else { 100 - wave };
        let radius = 100 + pulse;
        
        unsafe { core::ptr::write_bytes(info.fb_ptr, 0, info.height * info.stride * 4); }

        let cx = (info.width as isize) / 2;
        let cy = (info.height as isize) / 2;

        for y in 0..(info.height as isize) {
            for x in 0..(info.width as isize) {
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let pixel_index = (y as usize * info.stride + x as usize) * 4;
                    unsafe {
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index).write(255);       // B
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 1).write(100);   // G
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 2).write(pulse as u8 * 2); // R
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 3).write(255);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        time += 1;
        for _ in 0..1_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
EOF

#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct BootInfo {
    pub fb_ptr: *mut u8,
    pub width: usize,
    pub height: usize,
    pub stride: usize,
    pub app_ptr: *const u8,
}

const SIN_TABLE: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(info: &BootInfo) -> ! {
    let mut time: usize = 0;
    loop {
        unsafe { core::ptr::write_bytes(info.fb_ptr, 0, info.height * info.stride * 4); }
        let cx = (info.width as isize) / 2;
        let cy = (info.height as isize) / 2;
        let size: isize = 150;

        let angle = time % 36;
        let sin_a = SIN_TABLE[angle];
        let cos_a = SIN_TABLE[(angle + 9) % 36];

        for y in (cy - 200)..(cy + 200) {
            for x in (cx - 200)..(cx + 200) {
                if x < 0 || x >= info.width as isize || y < 0 || y >= info.height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let pixel_index = (y as usize * info.stride + x as usize) * 4;
                    unsafe {
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index).write(0);
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 1).write(255);
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 2).write(0);
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 3).write(255);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        time += 1;
        for _ in 0..1_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
EOF

# 3. ЗАГРУЗЧИК (BOOTLOADER)

cat << 'EOF' > $PROJECT_DIR/bootloader/Cargo.toml
[package]
name = "bootloader"
version = "0.1.0"
edition = "2021"

[dependencies]
uefi = "0.24.0"
uefi-services = "0.24.0"

[profile.release]
panic = "abort"
EOF

cat << 'EOF' > $PROJECT_DIR/bootloader/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use uefi::prelude::*;
use uefi::proto::console::gop::GraphicsOutput;
use uefi::table::boot::{AllocateType, MemoryType};

#[repr(C)]
pub struct BootInfo {
    pub fb_ptr: *mut u8,
    pub width: usize,
    pub height: usize,
    pub stride: usize,
    pub app_ptr: *const u8,
}

#[entry]
fn main(_image: Handle, mut system_table: SystemTable<Boot>) -> Status {
    uefi_services::init(&mut system_table).unwrap();
    let boot_services = system_table.boot_services();

    let kernel_bytes = include_bytes!("kernel.bin");
    let app_bytes = include_bytes!("app.bin");

    let kernel_pages = (kernel_bytes.len() / 4096) + 1;
    let kernel_addr = boot_services.allocate_pages(AllocateType::AnyPages, MemoryType::LOADER_DATA, kernel_pages).unwrap();
    unsafe { core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(kernel_bytes.as_ptr(), kernel_addr as *mut u8, kernel_bytes.len()); }

    let app_pages = (app_bytes.len() / 4096) + 1;
    let app_addr = boot_services.allocate_pages(AllocateType::AnyPages, MemoryType::LOADER_DATA, app_pages).unwrap();
    unsafe { core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(app_bytes.as_ptr(), app_addr as *mut u8, app_bytes.len()); }

    let gop_handle = boot_services.get_handle_for_protocol::<GraphicsOutput>().unwrap();
    let mut gop = boot_services.open_protocol_exclusive::<GraphicsOutput>(gop_handle).unwrap();
    let mode = gop.current_mode_info();
    
    let boot_info = BootInfo {
        fb_ptr: gop.frame_buffer().as_mut_ptr(),
        width: mode.resolution().0,
        height: mode.resolution().1,
        stride: mode.stride(),
        app_ptr: app_addr as *const u8,
    };

    let (_system_table, _memory_map) = system_table.exit_boot_services();
    let kernel_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(&BootInfo) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(kernel_addr) };
    kernel_entry(&boot_info);
}
EOF

#!/bin/bash
set -e

echo ">>> Проверка окружения..."
if ! command -v rustup &> /dev/null; then
    echo "Rust не найден. Запускаю установку..."
    curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -sSf https://sh.rustup.rs | sh -s -- -y
    source "$HOME/.cargo/env"
else
    echo "Rust установлен, обновляем..."
    rustup update
fi

echo ">>> Настройка Toolchain (Nightly) и таргетов..."
rustup default nightly
rustup target add x86_64-unknown-uefi x86_64-unknown-none
rustup component add llvm-tools-preview

echo ">>> Окружение готово к сборке!"

cd bootloader && cargo build --release --target x86_64-unknown-uefi && cd ..

echo ">>> [4/4] Сборка USB-образа..."
cp bootloader/target/x86_64-unknown-uefi/release/bootloader.efi usb_root/EFI/BOOT/BOOTX64.EFI

echo "=========================================================="
echo "СИСТЕМА СОБРАНА УСПЕШНО!"
echo "Артефакты находятся в usb_root/EFI/BOOT"
echo "=========================================================="

ENTRY(_start)
SECTIONS {
    . = 0x0; /* Относительный адрес, PIC код загрузится куда угодно */
    .text : {
        *(.text._start) /* Форсируем точку входа в самое начало бинарника! */
        *(.text .text.*)
    }
    .rodata : { *(.rodata .rodata.*) }
    .data : { *(.data .data.*) }
    .bss : { *(.bss .bss.*) }
    /DISCARD/ : { *(.eh_frame) }
}

cat << 'EOF' > kernel/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct BootInfo {
    pub fb_ptr: *mut u8,
    pub width: usize,
    pub height: usize,
    pub stride: usize,
    pub app_ptr: *const u8,
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(info: &BootInfo) -> ! {
    let mut time: usize = 0;

    loop {
        // Чтение порта клавиатуры PS/2
        let mut status: u8;
        let mut scancode: u8 = 0;
        unsafe {
            asm!("in al, 0x64", out("al") status);
            if (status & 1) == 1 { // Если буфер не пуст
                asm!("in al, 0x60", out("al") scancode);
            }
        }
        
        // 0x39 - сканкод Пробела (Spacebar)
        if scancode == 0x39 {
            // Handoff: Прыжок в Приложение
            let app_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(&BootInfo) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(info.app_ptr) };
            app_entry(info);
        }

        // Логика пульсирующей и волнующейся сферы
        let wave = (time % 100) as isize;
        let pulse = if wave < 50 { wave } else { 100 - wave };
        let radius = 100 + pulse;
        
        // Очистка экрана
        unsafe { core::ptr::write_bytes(info.fb_ptr, 0, info.height * info.stride * 4); }

        let cx = (info.width as isize) / 2;
        let cy = (info.height as isize) / 2;

        for y in 0..(info.height as isize) {
            for x in 0..(info.width as isize) {
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let pixel_index = (y as usize * info.stride + x as usize) * 4;
                    unsafe {
                        // Цветовая палитра Культуры
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index).write(255);       // B
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 1).write(100);   // G
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 2).write(pulse as u8 * 2); // R
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 3).write(255);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        time += 1;
        // Задержка CPU
        for _ in 0..1_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
    }
}

#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
EOF

# ==============================================================================

#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct BootInfo {
    pub fb_ptr: *mut u8,
    pub width: usize,
    pub height: usize,
    pub stride: usize,
    pub app_ptr: *const u8,
}

// Таблица синусов (умноженная на 100) для 36 углов (каждые 10 градусов)
const SIN_TABLE: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(info: &BootInfo) -> ! {
    let mut time: usize = 0;

    loop {
        unsafe { core::ptr::write_bytes(info.fb_ptr, 0, info.height * info.stride * 4); }

        let cx = (info.width as isize) / 2;
        let cy = (info.height as isize) / 2;
        let size: isize = 150;

        let angle = time % 36;
        let sin_a = SIN_TABLE[angle];
        let cos_a = SIN_TABLE[(angle + 9) % 36]; // cos(x) = sin(x + 90)

        // Отрисовка вращающегося квадрата
        for y in (cy - 200)..(cy + 200) {
            for x in (cx - 200)..(cx + 200) {
                if x < 0 || x >= info.width as isize || y < 0 || y >= info.height as isize { continue; }
                
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                // Обратное вращение координат пикселя
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let pixel_index = (y as usize * info.stride + x as usize) * 4;
                    unsafe {
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index).write(0);
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 1).write(255); // Neon Green
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 2).write(0);
                        info.fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 3).write(255);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        time += 1;
        for _ in 0..1_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
    }
}

#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
EOF

cat << 'EOF' > bootloader/Cargo.toml
[package]
name = "bootloader"
version = "0.1.0"
edition = "2021"

[dependencies]
uefi = "0.24.0"
uefi-services = "0.24.0"

[profile.release]
panic = "abort"

cat << 'EOF' > bootloader/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use uefi::prelude::*;
use uefi::proto::console::gop::GraphicsOutput;
use uefi::table::boot::{AllocateType, MemoryType};

#[repr(C)]
pub struct BootInfo {
    pub fb_ptr: *mut u8,
    pub width: usize,
    pub height: usize,
    pub stride: usize,
    pub app_ptr: *const u8,
}

#[entry]
fn main(_image: Handle, mut system_table: SystemTable<Boot>) -> Status {
    uefi_services::init(&mut system_table).unwrap();
    let boot_services = system_table.boot_services();

    // 1. Извлекаем бинарники (встроены прямо в файл загрузчика при компиляции!)
    let kernel_bytes = include_bytes!("kernel.bin");
    let app_bytes = include_bytes!("app.bin");

    // 2. Выделяем физическую память и копируем код
    let kernel_pages = (kernel_bytes.len() / 4096) + 1;
    let kernel_addr = boot_services.allocate_pages(AllocateType::AnyPages, MemoryType::LOADER_DATA, kernel_pages).unwrap();
    unsafe { core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(kernel_bytes.as_ptr(), kernel_addr as *mut u8, kernel_bytes.len()); }

    let app_pages = (app_bytes.len() / 4096) + 1;
    let app_addr = boot_services.allocate_pages(AllocateType::AnyPages, MemoryType::LOADER_DATA, app_pages).unwrap();
    unsafe { core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(app_bytes.as_ptr(), app_addr as *mut u8, app_bytes.len()); }

    // 3. Захват экрана
    let gop_handle = boot_services.get_handle_for_protocol::<GraphicsOutput>().unwrap();
    let mut gop = boot_services.open_protocol_exclusive::<GraphicsOutput>(gop_handle).unwrap();
    let mode = gop.current_mode_info();
    
    let boot_info = BootInfo {
        fb_ptr: gop.frame_buffer().as_mut_ptr(),
        width: mode.resolution().0,
        height: mode.resolution().1,
        stride: mode.stride(),
        app_ptr: app_addr as *const u8,
    };

    // 4. РУБИКОН. Выключаем UEFI.
    let (_system_table, _memory_map) = system_table.exit_boot_services();

    // 5. Прыжок в Ядро!
    let kernel_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(&BootInfo) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(kernel_addr) };
    kernel_entry(&boot_info);
}
EOF

echo ">>> [3/7] Компиляция Ядра и Приложения (ELF)..."
cd kernel && cargo build --release && cd ..
cd app && cargo build --release && cd ..

echo ">>> [4/7] Конвертация в плоские бинарники (Raw Binaries)..."

$OBJCOPY -O binary kernel/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/kernel bootloader/src/kernel.bin
$OBJCOPY -O binary app/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/app bootloader/src/app.bin

echo ">>> [5/7] Компиляция Загрузчика (PE/COFF)..."
cd bootloader
cargo build --release --target x86_64-unknown-uefi

=== file update_all.sh content:
#!/bin/bash
set -e

# Явно подключаем окружение Rust, чтобы терминал увидел cargo
source "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null || true
export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH"

ROOT_DIR=$(pwd)

echo ">>> [1/3] Обновление Ядра (kernel/src/main.rs)..."
cat << 'INNER_KERNEL' > kernel/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'.' => row >= 6 && col >= 3 && col <= 4,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        if ascii != b' ' {
            draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        }
        cx += 10;
    }
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    unsafe { asm!("cli"); }
    let total_pixels = height * stride;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1; 
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let w_isize = width as isize;
    let h_isize = height as isize;

    let mut time: usize = 0;
    let mut wave: usize = 0;
    let timeout_frames = 600; 

    loop {
        let mut status: u8;
        let mut scancode: u8 = 0;
        unsafe {
            asm!("in al, 0x64", out("al") status);
            if (status & 1) == 1 { 
                asm!("in al, 0x60", out("al") scancode); 
            }
        }

        if time > timeout_frames || (scancode != 0 && scancode < 0x80) {
            for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00FFFFFF); } }
            for _ in 0..10_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
            
            let app_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, *mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const u8) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(app_ptr) };
            app_entry(fb_ptr, bb_ptr, width, height, stride, core::ptr::null());
        }

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00000000); } }

        let phase = (wave / 3) % 100;
        let pulse = if phase < 50 { phase as isize } else { (100 - phase) as isize };
        let radius = 100 + (pulse / 2);
        
        for y in 0..h_isize {
            for x in 0..w_isize {
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let offset = (y as usize) * stride + (x as usize);
                    let color = 0xFF000000 | (((pulse * 3) as u32) << 16) | (140 << 8) | 255;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), color); }
                }
            }
        }

        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 50, b"MIND CORE", 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 80, b"ZEN", 0x0000FF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        
        wave += 1;
        time += 1;

        for _ in 0..4_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

const SIN_TABLE: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'.' => row >= 6 && col >= 3 && col <= 4,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        if ascii != b' ' {
            draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        }
        cx += 10;
    }
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, _app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    let mut frame: usize = 0;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    loop {
        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 150;
        let mut t_temp = frame / 12; 
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = SIN_TABLE[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = SIN_TABLE[cos_angle];

        for y in (cy - 200)..(cy + 200) {
            for x in (cx - 200)..(cx + 200) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), 0xFF00FF00); }
                }
            }
        }

        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 50, b"USERSPACE", 0x0000FF00);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 80, b"APP ACTIVE", 0x00AAAAAA);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        
        frame += 1;
        for _ in 0..1_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }

#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

// Определение структуры системных вызовов (API Операционной Системы)
#[repr(C)]
pub struct SystemApi {
    pub draw_string_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const u8, usize, u32),
    pub get_ticks_fn: extern "sysv64" fn() -> usize,
    pub read_file_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*const u8, usize, *mut u8, usize) -> usize,
}

// Встроенная виртуальная файловая система в памяти (RAMFS)
struct VirtualFile {
    name: &'static [u8],
    content: &'static [u8],
}

const RAMFS: &[VirtualFile] = &[
    VirtualFile {
        name: b"welcome.txt",
        content: b"MIND CORE OS v0.9 - MICROKERNEL RAMFS ACTIVE.",
    },
    VirtualFile {
        name: b"sysinfo.cfg",
        content: b"ARCH=X86_64 MODE=BARE_METAL SEC_LEVEL=RING0_3",
    },
];

// Реализация системных вызовов ядра
extern "sysv64" fn sys_draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text_ptr: *const u8, text_len: usize, color: u32) {
    if text_ptr.is_null() { return; }
    let text = unsafe { core::slice::from_raw_parts(text_ptr, text_len) };
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        if ascii != b' ' {
            draw_char_internal(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        }
        cx += 10;
    }
}

static mut SYSTEM_TICKS: usize = 0;

extern "sysv64" fn sys_get_ticks() -> usize {
    unsafe { SYSTEM_TICKS }
}

extern "sysv64" fn sys_read_file(name_ptr: *const u8, name_len: usize, buf: *mut u8, buf_len: usize) -> usize {
    if name_ptr.is_null() || buf.is_null() { return 0; }
    let name = unsafe { core::slice::from_raw_parts(name_ptr, name_len) };
    
    for file in RAMFS {
        if file.name == name {
            let copy_len = if file.content.len() < buf_len { file.content.len() } else { buf_len };
            unsafe {
                core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(file.content.as_ptr(), buf, copy_len);
            }
            return copy_len;
        }
    }
    0
}

fn draw_char_internal(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 || row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0'..=b'9' => row == 0 || row == 7 || col == 0 || col == 7,
                b'=' => row == 2 || row == 5,
                b'-' => row == 3,
                b'.' => row >= 6 && col >= 3 && col <= 4,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    unsafe { asm!("cli"); }
    let total_pixels = height * stride;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1; 
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let w_isize = width as isize;
    let h_isize = height as isize;

    let mut time: usize = 0;
    let mut wave: usize = 0;
    let timeout_frames = 600; 

    // Формируем таблицу API для передачи в Userspace
    let api = SystemApi {
        draw_string_fn: sys_draw_string,
        get_ticks_fn: sys_get_ticks,
        read_file_fn: sys_read_file,
    };

    loop {
        unsafe { SYSTEM_TICKS = time; }
        
        let mut status: u8;
        let mut scancode: u8 = 0;
        unsafe {
            asm!("in al, 0x64", out("al") status);
            if (status & 1) == 1 { 
                asm!("in al, 0x60", out("al") scancode); 
            }
        }

        if time > timeout_frames || (scancode != 0 && scancode < 0x80) {
            for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00FFFFFF); } }
            for _ in 0..10_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
            
            // Прыжок в Приложение с передачей указателя на таблицу API в регистре RDX (по соглашению SysV)
            let app_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, *mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const SystemApi) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(app_ptr) };
            app_entry(fb_ptr, bb_ptr, width, height, stride, &api as *const SystemApi);
        }

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00000000); } }

        let phase = (wave / 3) % 100;
        let pulse = if phase < 50 { phase as isize } else { (100 - phase) as isize };
        let radius = 100 + (pulse / 2);
        
        for y in 0..h_isize {
            for x in 0..w_isize {
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let offset = (y as usize) * stride + (x as usize);
                    let color = 0xFF000000 | (((pulse * 3) as u32) <= 16) as u32 | (140 << 8) | 255;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), color); }
                }
            }
        }

        sys_draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 50, b"MIND CORE OS", 12, 0x00FFFFFF);
        sys_draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 80, b"ZEN SPHERE", 10, 0x0000FF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        
        wave += 1;
        time += 1;
        for _ in 0..2_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }

echo ">>> [2/3] Создание Userspace ПРИЛОЖЕНИЯ с вызовами ОС, файлами и счетчиком циклов..."
cat << 'INNER_APP' > app/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SystemApi {
    pub draw_string_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const u8, usize, u32),
    pub get_ticks_fn: extern "sysv64" fn() -> usize,
    pub read_file_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*const u8, usize, *mut u8, usize) -> usize,
}

const SIN_TABLE: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];

// Функция для перевода числа в строку (чтобы выводить счетчик на экран)
fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 {
        buf[0] = b'0';
        return 1;
    }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx {
        buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i];
    }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, api_ptr: *const SystemApi) -> ! {
    let api = unsafe { &*api_ptr };
    
    // Читаем файл из RAMFS через системный вызов ядра при старте приложения!
    let mut file_buf = [0u8; 64];
    let filename = b"welcome.txt";
    let bytes_read = (api.read_file_fn)(filename.as_ptr(), filename.len(), file_buf.as_mut_ptr(), file_buf.len());
    let file_str = if bytes_read > 0 { &file_buf[..bytes_read] } else { b"FILE NOT FOUND" };

    let mut frame: usize = 0;
    let mut cycle_counter: usize = 0;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    loop {
        cycle_counter += 1; // Максимально быстрый счетчик циклов рендеринга

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 120;
        // Плавное вращение квадрата
        let mut t_temp = frame / 8; 
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = SIN_TABLE[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = SIN_TABLE[cos_angle];

        for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
            for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), 0xFF00FF00); }
                }
            }
        }

        // Вызов системных функций ОС для рендеринга интерфейса
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, b"USERSPACE APP RUNNING", 21, 0x0000FF00);
        
        // Рендерим содержимое файла, прочитанного из RAMFS
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, file_str.as_ptr(), file_str.len(), 0x0000FFFF);

        // Превращаем счетчик циклов в текст и выводим на экран в реальном времени
        let mut num_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let len = usize_to_str(cycle_counter, &mut num_buf);
        
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 100, b"CYCLES:", 7, 0x00FFFFFF);
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 100, num_buf.as_ptr(), len, 0x00FFFF00);

        // Swap buffers
        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        
        frame += 1;
        // Убрали искусственные задержки (nop), чтобы счетчик крутился на максимальной скорости процессора!
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_APP

cd "$ROOT_DIR/kernel" && cargo build --release
cd "$ROOT_DIR/app" && cargo build --release

$OBJCOPY -O binary -j .text -j .rodata -j .data "$ROOT_DIR/kernel/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/kernel" "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/src/kernel.bin"
$OBJCOPY -O binary -j .text -j .rodata -j .data "$ROOT_DIR/app/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/app" "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/src/app.bin"

cd "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader" && cargo build --release --target x86_64-unknown-uefi

echo "=========================================================="

=== file: legacy/update_os_v3_stable.sh
=== file update_os_v3_stable.sh content:
#!/bin/bash
set -e
source "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null || true
export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH"
ROOT_DIR=$(pwd)

echo ">>> [1/3] ЯДРО: Защищенная таблица API (Static Data) и полный шрифт..."
cat << 'INNER_KERNEL' > kernel/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SystemApi {
    pub draw_string_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const u8, usize, u32),
    pub get_ticks_fn: extern "sysv64" fn() -> usize,
    pub read_file_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*const u8, usize, *mut u8, usize) -> usize,
}

struct VirtualFile {
    name: &'static [u8],
    content: &'static [u8],
}

const RAMFS: &[VirtualFile] = &[
    VirtualFile {
        name: b"welcome.txt",
        content: b"MIND CORE OS v1.0 - RING 3 READY.",
    },
];

extern "sysv64" fn sys_draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text_ptr: *const u8, text_len: usize, color: u32) {
    if text_ptr.is_null() { return; }
    let text = unsafe { core::slice::from_raw_parts(text_ptr, text_len) };
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        if ascii != b' ' {
            draw_char_internal(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        }
        cx += 10;
    }
}

static mut SYSTEM_TICKS: usize = 0;
extern "sysv64" fn sys_get_ticks() -> usize { unsafe { SYSTEM_TICKS } }

extern "sysv64" fn sys_read_file(name_ptr: *const u8, name_len: usize, buf: *mut u8, buf_len: usize) -> usize {
    if name_ptr.is_null() || buf.is_null() { return 0; }
    let name = unsafe { core::slice::from_raw_parts(name_ptr, name_len) };
    for file in RAMFS {
        if file.name == name {
            let copy_len = if file.content.len() < buf_len { file.content.len() } else { buf_len };
            unsafe { core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(file.content.as_ptr(), buf, copy_len); }
            return copy_len;
        }
    }
    0
}

// СТАТИЧЕСКАЯ ТАБЛИЦА API (Лежит в .rodata, защищена от перезаписи стеком Приложения)
static KERNEL_API: SystemApi = SystemApi {
    draw_string_fn: sys_draw_string,
    get_ticks_fn: sys_get_ticks,
    read_file_fn: sys_read_file,
};

fn draw_char_internal(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0'..=b'9' => row == 0 || row == 7 || col == 0 || col == 7,
                b':' => row == 2 || row == 5,
                b'-' => row == 3,
                b'.' => row >= 6 && col >= 3 && col <= 4,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    unsafe { asm!("cli"); }
    let total_pixels = height * stride;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1; 
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let w_isize = width as isize;
    let h_isize = height as isize;

    let mut time: usize = 0;
    let mut wave: usize = 0;
    let timeout_frames = 200; // Быстрый тест

    loop {
        unsafe { SYSTEM_TICKS = time; }
        
        let mut status: u8;
        let mut scancode: u8 = 0;
        unsafe {
            asm!("in al, 0x64", out("al") status);
            if (status & 1) == 1 { asm!("in al, 0x60", out("al") scancode); }
        }

        if time > timeout_frames || (scancode != 0 && scancode < 0x80) {
            for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00FFFFFF); } }
            for _ in 0..5_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
            
            // Прыжок с передачей указателя на СТАТИЧЕСКУЮ таблицу
            let app_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, *mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const SystemApi) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(app_ptr) };
            app_entry(fb_ptr, bb_ptr, width, height, stride, &KERNEL_API);
        }

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00000000); } }

        let phase = (wave / 3) % 100;
        let pulse = if phase < 50 { phase as isize } else { (100 - phase) as isize };
        let radius = 100 + (pulse / 2);
        
        for y in 0..h_isize {
            for x in 0..w_isize {
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let offset = (y as usize) * stride + (x as usize);
                    let color = 0xFF000000 | (((pulse * 3) as u32) << 16) | (140 << 8) | 255;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), color); }
                }
            }
        }

        sys_draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 50, b"MIND CORE OS".as_ptr(), 12, 0x00FFFFFF);
        sys_draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 80, b"ZEN SPHERE".as_ptr(), 10, 0x0000FF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        wave += 1;
        time += 1;
        for _ in 0..3_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_KERNEL

#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::panic::PanicInfo;

// Структура на 100% идентична Ядру
#[repr(C)]
pub struct SystemApi {
    pub draw_string_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const u8, usize, u32),
    pub get_ticks_fn: extern "sysv64" fn() -> usize,
    pub read_file_fn: extern "sysv64" fn(*const u8, usize, *mut u8, usize) -> usize,
}

const SIN_TABLE: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];

fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, api_ptr: *const SystemApi) -> ! {
    let api = unsafe { &*api_ptr };
    
    let mut file_buf = [0u8; 64];
    let filename = b"welcome.txt";
    let bytes_read = (api.read_file_fn)(filename.as_ptr(), filename.len(), file_buf.as_mut_ptr(), file_buf.len());
    let file_str = if bytes_read > 0 { &file_buf[..bytes_read] } else { b"FILE NOT FOUND" };

    let mut frame: usize = 0;
    let mut cycle_counter: usize = 0;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    loop {
        cycle_counter += 1;

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 120;
        let mut t_temp = frame / 10; 
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = SIN_TABLE[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = SIN_TABLE[cos_angle];

        for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
            for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), 0xFF00FF00); }
                }
            }
        }

        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, b"USERSPACE APP".as_ptr(), 13, 0x0000FF00);
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, file_str.as_ptr(), file_str.len(), 0x0000FFFF);

        let mut num_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let len = usize_to_str(cycle_counter, &mut num_buf);
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 100, b"CYCLES:".as_ptr(), 7, 0x00FFFFFF);
        (api.draw_string_fn)(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 100, num_buf.as_ptr(), len, 0x00FFFF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        frame += 1;
    }
}
