=== file: legacy/update_us.sh
=== file update_us.sh content:
#!/bin/bash
set -e

PROJECT_NAME="mind_core"
cd $PROJECT_NAME

echo ">>> Обновление исходного кода ядра (src/main.rs)..."
cat << 'EOF' > src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::char;
use uefi::prelude::*;
use uefi::proto::console::gop::GraphicsOutput;
use uefi::proto::console::text::Key;

// Выделенная функция для перерисовки круга с заданным цветом (RGB)
fn draw_circle(fb_ptr: *mut u8, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, radius: isize, r: u8, g: u8, b: u8) {
    let center_x = width as isize / 2;
    let center_y = height as isize / 2;

    for y in 0..(height as isize) {
        for x in 0..(width as isize) {
            let dx = x - center_x;
            let dy = y - center_y;
            
            if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                let pixel_index = (y as usize * stride + x as usize) * 4;
                unsafe {
                    fb_ptr.add(pixel_index).write(b);       // Blue
                    fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 1).write(g);   // Green
                    fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 2).write(r);   // Red
                    fb_ptr.add(pixel_index + 3).write(255); // Alpha
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

#[entry]
fn main(image: Handle, mut system_table: SystemTable<Boot>) -> Status {
    uefi_services::init(&mut system_table).unwrap();
    let boot_services = system_table.boot_services();

    let gop_handle = boot_services.get_handle_for_protocol::<GraphicsOutput>().unwrap();
    let mut gop = boot_services.open_protocol_exclusive::<GraphicsOutput>(gop_handle).unwrap();

    let mode_info = gop.current_mode_info();
    let (width, height) = mode_info.resolution();
    let stride = mode_info.stride();

    let mut fb = gop.frame_buffer();
    let fb_ptr = fb.as_mut_ptr();

    // Первичная отрисовка синего круга при загрузке
    draw_circle(fb_ptr, width, height, stride, 150, 0, 0, 255);

    // Главный цикл (event loop)
    loop {
        // Неблокирующий опрос буфера клавиатуры
        if let Ok(Some(key)) = system_table.stdin().read_key() {
            if let Key::Printable(char16) = key {
                // Безопасное приведение UEFI-символа к стандартному char
                let ch = char::from_u32(u16::from(char16) as u32).unwrap_or(' ');
                
                // Смена цвета (пиксельного буфера) по нажатию
                match ch {
                    'r' | 'R' => draw_circle(fb_ptr, width, height, stride, 150, 255, 0, 0),
                    'g' | 'G' => draw_circle(fb_ptr, width, height, stride, 150, 0, 255, 0),
                    'b' | 'B' => draw_circle(fb_ptr, width, height, stride, 150, 0, 0, 255),
                    'y' | 'Y' => draw_circle(fb_ptr, width, height, stride, 150, 255, 255, 0),
                    'w' | 'W' => draw_circle(fb_ptr, width, height, stride, 150, 255, 255, 255),
                    _ => {} // Игнорируем остальные нажатия
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
EOF

echo ">>> Компиляция интерактивного ядра..."
cargo build --target x86_64-unknown-uefi --release

echo ">>> Обновление образа на флешке..."
cp target/x86_64-unknown-uefi/release/mind_core.efi ../usb_root/EFI/BOOT/BOOTX64.EFI

echo ""
echo "Готово! Папка usb_root обновлена. Замените файлы на флешке и перезагрузитесь."

=== end of file update_us.sh content

=== file: patches_seq/README.md
=== file README.md content:
# patches_seq/

Последовательность патчей, каждый из которых целиком перезаписывает
затрагиваемые исходники и наращивает функциональность ядра.

| Патч | Содержание |
|---|---|
| `patch_001_idt_timer.sh` | Инициализация IDT и аппаратного таймера PIT |
| `patch_002_idt_fix.sh` | Исправление компиляции IDT и ABI обработчика |
| `patch_003_syscalls.sh` | `int 0x80`, разделение счётчиков, фикс вращения |
| `patch_004_scheduler.sh` | Кооперативный планировщик и переключение контекста |
| `patch_004_fixed.sh` | Исправленная редакция патча 004 |
| `patch_005_rust_1_88_fixes.sh` | Совместимость с Rust 1.88 |
| `patch_006_keyboard_irq.sh` | Аппаратные прерывания клавиатуры, интерактивное API |
| `patch_008_preemptive.sh` | Вытесняющая многозадачность в ядре |
| `patch_010_debug_console.sh` | Интерактивная консоль ядра (REPL) |
| `patch_011_com_port.sh` | Вывод в COM-порт |

Патчи 001–008 перенесены из `Culture/legacy/v3` (2026-09-15); 010 и 011
написаны позже, уже в этом дереве. Патчи 007 и 009 в истории отсутствуют.

Патчи 001–008 рассчитаны на дерево от 15 сентября (передача управления
через 6 аргументов SysV и двойная буферизация). Текущие исходники
используют структуру `BootInfo`, поэтому применение старых патчей поверх
них требует ручной сверки. Более ранние одноразовые генераторы — в
[../legacy/](../legacy/).
=== end of file README.md content

=== file: patches_seq/new_patch.sh
=== file new_patch.sh content:
#!/bin/bash
# new_patch.sh — создаёт следующий по номеру патч, принимает его текст со
# стандартного ввода и сразу применяет.
#
# Использование:
#   ./new_patch.sh              создать patch_<max+1>.sh, ввести текст, применить
#   ./new_patch.sh -t           начать с шаблона-заголовка (SCRIPT_DIR/ROOT_DIR)
#   ./new_patch.sh -n           только создать файл, не запускать
#
# Текст патча вводится до Ctrl-D. Пустой ввод отменяет создание.

set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
ROOT_DIR="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/.." && pwd)"

USE_TEMPLATE=0
RUN_AFTER=1

while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
    case "$1" in
        -t|--template) USE_TEMPLATE=1 ;;
        -n|--no-run)   RUN_AFTER=0 ;;
        -h|--help)     sed -n '2,12p' "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}" | sed 's/^# \?//'; exit 0 ;;
        *) echo "Неизвестный аргумент: $1" >&2; exit 1 ;;
    esac
    shift
done

# --- Определяем максимальный номер ---------------------------------------

max=0
for f in "$SCRIPT_DIR"/patch_*.sh; do
    [ -e "$f" ] || continue
    n=$(basename "$f" | sed -n 's/^patch_0*\([0-9]\+\).*/\1/p')
    [ -n "$n" ] || continue
    [ "$n" -gt "$max" ] && max=$n
done

# Если последний патч пуст — переиспользуем его номер вместо нового.
reuse=""
if [ "$max" -gt 0 ]; then
    last=$(printf '%s/patch_%03d' "$SCRIPT_DIR" "$max")
    for cand in "$last".sh "$last"_*.sh; do
        if [ -f "$cand" ] && [ ! -s "$cand" ]; then
            reuse="$cand"
            break
        fi
    done
fi

if [ -n "$reuse" ]; then
    NUM=$max
    PATCH_FILE="$reuse"
    echo "Найден пустой патч $(basename "$PATCH_FILE") — заполняем его."
else
    NUM=$((max + 1))
    PATCH_FILE=$(printf '%s/patch_%03d.sh' "$SCRIPT_DIR" "$NUM")
fi

if [ -s "$PATCH_FILE" ]; then
    echo "Ошибка: $PATCH_FILE уже существует и непуст." >&2
    exit 1
fi

# --- Приглашение и ввод ---------------------------------------------------

printf 'Максимальный номер патча: %03d\n' "$max"
printf 'Новый патч: %s\n' "$(basename "$PATCH_FILE")"
echo "Вводите текст патча. Завершение — Ctrl-D, отмена — пустой ввод."
echo "----------------------------------------------------------------"
printf 'cat > %s\n' "$PATCH_FILE"

TMP=$(mktemp)
trap 'rm -f "$TMP"' EXIT

if [ "$USE_TEMPLATE" = 1 ]; then
    cat > "$TMP" <<TEMPLATE
#!/bin/bash
set -e

SCRIPT_DIR="\$(cd "\$(dirname "\${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
ROOT_DIR="\$(cd "\$SCRIPT_DIR/.." && pwd)"

echo ">>> Применяем Патч $(printf '%03d' "$NUM"): ..."

TEMPLATE
fi

cat >> "$TMP"

echo "----------------------------------------------------------------"

# Порог "пусто": без шаблона — ноль байт, с шаблоном — только сам шаблон.
min_lines=0
[ "$USE_TEMPLATE" = 1 ] && min_lines=8

if [ "$(wc -l < "$TMP")" -le "$min_lines" ]; then
    echo "Содержимое не введено — патч не создан."
    exit 0
fi

cp "$TMP" "$PATCH_FILE"
echo "Записано: $PATCH_FILE ($(wc -l < "$PATCH_FILE") строк)"

# --- Проверка синтаксиса и запуск ----------------------------------------

if ! bash -n "$PATCH_FILE"; then
    echo "Синтаксическая ошибка в патче — запуск отменён." >&2
    exit 1
fi

if [ "$RUN_AFTER" = 0 ]; then
    echo "Запуск пропущен (-n). Применить вручную: bash $PATCH_FILE"
    exit 0
fi

echo ">>> Запуск $(basename "$PATCH_FILE") из $ROOT_DIR ..."
cd "$ROOT_DIR"
bash "$PATCH_FILE"
=== end of file new_patch.sh content

=== file: patches_seq/patch_001_idt_timer.sh
=== file patch_001_idt_timer.sh content:
#!/bin/bash
set -e
source "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null || true
export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH"
ROOT_DIR=$(pwd)

echo ">>> Применяем Патч 001: Инициализация IDT и аппаратного таймера PIT..."

cat << 'INNER_KERNEL' > kernel/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]
#![feature(abi_x86_interrupt)] // Разрешаем встроенный ABI для обработчиков прерываний

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

// --- ПОРТЫ ВВОДА/ВЫВОДА ---
unsafe fn outb(port: u16, val: u8) {
    asm!("out dx, al", in("dx") port, in("al") val, options(nomem, nostack));
}

// --- IDT И ПРЕРЫВАНИЯ ---
#[repr(C, packed)]
struct IdtEntry {
    offset_low: u16,
    selector: u16,
    ist: u8,
    type_attr: u8,
    offset_mid: u16,
    offset_high: u32,
    zero: u32,
}

#[repr(C, packed)]
struct IdtPtr {
    limit: u16,
    base: u64,
}

static mut IDT: [IdtEntry; 256] = [IdtEntry { offset_low: 0, selector: 0, ist: 0, type_attr: 0, offset_mid: 0, offset_high: 0, zero: 0 }; 256];
static mut SYSTEM_TICKS: usize = 0;

// Аппаратный обработчик прерывания таймера
extern "x86-interrupt" fn timer_handler() {
    unsafe {
        SYSTEM_TICKS += 1;
        outb(0x20, 0x20); // Отправляем сигнал EOI (End of Interrupt) в контроллер PIC
    }
}

unsafe fn get_cs() -> u16 {
    let mut cs: u16;
    asm!("mov {:x}, cs", out(reg) cs);
    cs
}

unsafe fn init_idt() {
    let handler_addr = timer_handler as u64;
    let cs = get_cs(); // Читаем текущий сегмент кода, оставленный UEFI
    
    // Настраиваем 32-й вектор (IRQ0 - Таймер)
    IDT[32].offset_low = handler_addr as u16;
    IDT[32].selector = cs;
    IDT[32].ist = 0;
    IDT[32].type_attr = 0x8E; // Шлюз прерывания, Present
    IDT[32].offset_mid = (handler_addr >> 16) as u16;
    IDT[32].offset_high = (handler_addr >> 32) as u32;

    let idt_ptr = IdtPtr {
        limit: (core::mem::size_of::<[IdtEntry; 256]>() - 1) as u16,
        base: IDT.as_ptr() as u64,
    };
    asm!("lidt [{}]", in(reg) &idt_ptr);
}

unsafe fn init_pic_pit() {
    // Перепрограммируем контроллер прерываний PIC, чтобы IRQ не конфликтовали с исключениями CPU (сдвигаем на 0x20)
    outb(0x20, 0x11); outb(0xA0, 0x11);
    outb(0x21, 0x20); outb(0xA1, 0x28);
    outb(0x21, 0x04); outb(0xA1, 0x02);
    outb(0x21, 0x01); outb(0xA1, 0x01);
    
    // Оставляем включенным ТОЛЬКО таймер (маска 0xFE = 11111110)
    outb(0x21, 0xFE); outb(0xA1, 0xFF); 

    // Настраиваем PIT таймер на 1000 Гц (1 миллисекунда)
    let divisor: u32 = 1193182 / 1000;
    outb(0x43, 0x36);
    outb(0x40, (divisor & 0xFF) as u8);
    outb(0x40, ((divisor >> 8) & 0xFF) as u8);
}

// --- ГРАФИКА ---
fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0' => (row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7,
                b'1' => col == 4 || (row == 1 && col == 3) || row == 7,
                b'2' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 3 && row < 7) || row == 7,
                b'3' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 7 && row != 3,
                b'4' => col == 6 || (row == 4) || (col == 1 && row < 4) || (row + col == 6 && col < 6),
                b'5' => row == 0 || (col == 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7),
                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => (row == 2 || row == 5) && (col == 3 || col == 4),
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    unsafe { 
        asm!("cli"); // Запрещаем прерывания на время настройки
        init_idt();
        init_pic_pit();
        asm!("sti"); // Разрешаем прерывания (теперь таймер начнет тикать аппаратно!)
    }

    let total_pixels = height * stride;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1; 
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let w_isize = width as isize;
    let h_isize = height as isize;

    let mut wave: usize = 0;

    loop {
        // Читаем текущий аппаратный тик таймера
        let current_ticks = unsafe { core::ptr::read_volatile(&SYSTEM_TICKS) };

        let mut status: u8;
        let mut scancode: u8 = 0;
        unsafe {
            asm!("in al, 0x64", out("al") status);
            if (status & 1) == 1 { asm!("in al, 0x60", out("al") scancode); }
        }

        // Переходим в Приложение по нажатию клавиши ИЛИ после ~5 секунд аппаратного таймера (5000 мс)
        if current_ticks > 5000 || (scancode != 0 && scancode < 0x80) {
            for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00FFFFFF); } }
            for _ in 0..5_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
            
            let app_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, *mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const usize) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(app_ptr) };
            // ПЕРЕДАЕМ ПРИЛОЖЕНИЮ АДРЕС НАШЕГО АППАРАТНОГО СЧЕТЧИКА!
            app_entry(fb_ptr, bb_ptr, width, height, stride, unsafe { &SYSTEM_TICKS as *const usize });
        }

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00000000); } }

        let phase = (wave / 3) % 100;
        let pulse = if phase < 50 { phase as isize } else { (100 - phase) as isize };
        let radius = 100 + (pulse / 2);
        
        for y in 0..h_isize {
            for x in 0..w_isize {
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let offset = (y as usize) * stride + (x as usize);
                    let color = 0xFF000000 | (((pulse * 3) as u32) << 16) | (140 << 8) | 255;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), color); }
                }
            }
        }

        let msg_core = [b'M', b'I', b'N', b'D', b' ', b'C', b'O', b'R', b'E'];
        let msg_time = [b'H', b'W', b' ', b'T', b'I', b'M', b'E', b'R', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 50, &msg_core, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 80, &msg_time, 0x0000FF00);
        
        let mut t_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let t_len = usize_to_str(current_ticks, &mut t_buf);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 150, 80, &t_buf[0..t_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        wave += 1;
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_KERNEL

cat << 'INNER_APP' > app/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::panic::PanicInfo;

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0' => (row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7,
                b'1' => col == 4 || (row == 1 && col == 3) || row == 7,
                b'2' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 3 && row < 7) || row == 7,
                b'3' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 7 && row != 3,
                b'4' => col == 6 || (row == 4) || (col == 1 && row < 4) || (row + col == 6 && col < 6),
                b'5' => row == 0 || (col == 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7),
                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => (row == 2 || row == 5) && (col == 3 || col == 4),
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, ticks_ptr: *const usize) -> ! {
    let sin_table: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];
    
    let mut frame_counter: usize = 0;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    loop {
        frame_counter += 1;

        // Читаем значение аппаратного таймера по указателю от Ядра!
        let hardware_time = unsafe { core::ptr::read_volatile(ticks_ptr) };

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 120;
        
        // Делитель для медленного и плавного вращения квадрата
        let mut t_temp = frame_counter / 20; 
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = sin_table[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = sin_table[cos_angle];

        for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
            for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), 0xFF00FF00); }
                }
            }
        }

        let app_msg = [b'U', b'S', b'E', b'R', b'S', b'P', b'A', b'C', b'E'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, &app_msg, 0x0000FF00);

        // 1. Вывод программных кадров (итераций)
        let mut f_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let f_len = usize_to_str(frame_counter, &mut f_buf);
        let f_msg = [b'F', b'R', b'A', b'M', b'E', b'S', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, &f_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 70, &f_buf[0..f_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        // 2. Вывод аппаратного миллисекундного таймера (из Ядра)
        let mut w_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let w_len = usize_to_str(hardware_time, &mut w_buf);
        let w_msg = [b'M', b'S', b'E', b'C', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 100, &w_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 100, &w_buf[0..w_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_APP

echo ">>> Сборка проекта..."
cd "$ROOT_DIR/kernel" && cargo build --release
cd "$ROOT_DIR/app" && cargo build --release

OBJCOPY=$(find $(rustc --print sysroot) -name llvm-objcopy | head -n 1)
$OBJCOPY -O binary -j .text -j .rodata -j .data "$ROOT_DIR/kernel/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/kernel" "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/src/kernel.bin"
$OBJCOPY -O binary -j .text -j .rodata -j .data "$ROOT_DIR/app/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/app" "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/src/app.bin"

cd "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader" && cargo build --release --target x86_64-unknown-uefi
cp "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/target/x86_64-unknown-uefi/release/bootloader.efi" "$ROOT_DIR/usb_root/EFI/BOOT/BOOTX64.EFI"

=== file: patches_seq/patch_002_idt_fix.sh
=== file patch_002_idt_fix.sh content:
#!/bin/bash
set -e
source "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null || true
export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH"
ROOT_DIR=$(pwd)

echo ">>> Применяем Патч 002: Исправление компиляции IDT и ABI обработчика..."

cat << 'INNER_KERNEL' > kernel/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]
#![feature(abi_x86_interrupt)]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

unsafe fn outb(port: u16, val: u8) {
    asm!("out dx, al", in("dx") port, in("al") val, options(nomem, nostack));
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
#[repr(C, packed)]
struct IdtEntry {
    offset_low: u16,
    selector: u16,
    ist: u8,
    type_attr: u8,
    offset_mid: u16,
    offset_high: u32,
    zero: u32,
}

#[repr(C, packed)]
struct IdtPtr {
    limit: u16,
    base: u64,
}

#[repr(C)]
pub struct InterruptFrame {
    pub rip: u64,
    pub cs: u64,
    pub rflags: u64,
    pub rsp: u64,
    pub ss: u64,
}

static mut IDT: [IdtEntry; 256] = [IdtEntry { offset_low: 0, selector: 0, ist: 0, type_attr: 0, offset_mid: 0, offset_high: 0, zero: 0 }; 256];
static mut SYSTEM_TICKS: usize = 0;

extern "x86-interrupt" fn timer_handler(_frame: &mut InterruptFrame) {
    unsafe {
        SYSTEM_TICKS += 1;
        outb(0x20, 0x20); // EOI
    }
}

unsafe fn get_cs() -> u16 {
    let mut cs: u16;
    asm!("mov ax, cs", out("ax") cs, options(nomem, nostack));
    cs
}

unsafe fn init_idt() {
    let handler_addr = timer_handler as u64;
    let cs = get_cs(); 
    
    IDT[32].offset_low = handler_addr as u16;
    IDT[32].selector = cs;
    IDT[32].ist = 0;
    IDT[32].type_attr = 0x8E;
    IDT[32].offset_mid = (handler_addr >> 16) as u16;
    IDT[32].offset_high = (handler_addr >> 32) as u32;

    let idt_ptr = IdtPtr {
        limit: (core::mem::size_of::<[IdtEntry; 256]>() - 1) as u16,
        base: IDT.as_ptr() as u64,
    };
    asm!("lidt [{}]", in(reg) &idt_ptr);
}

unsafe fn init_pic_pit() {
    outb(0x20, 0x11); outb(0xA0, 0x11);
    outb(0x21, 0x20); outb(0xA1, 0x28);
    outb(0x21, 0x04); outb(0xA1, 0x02);
    outb(0x21, 0x01); outb(0xA1, 0x01);
    
    outb(0x21, 0xFE); outb(0xA1, 0xFF); 

    let divisor: u32 = 1193182 / 1000;
    outb(0x43, 0x36);
    outb(0x40, (divisor & 0xFF) as u8);
    outb(0x40, ((divisor >> 8) & 0xFF) as u8);
}

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0' => (row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7,
                b'1' => col == 4 || (row == 1 && col == 3) || row == 7,
                b'2' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 3 && row < 7) || row == 7,
                b'3' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 7 && row != 3,
                b'4' => col == 6 || (row == 4) || (col == 1 && row < 4) || (row + col == 6 && col < 6),
                b'5' => row == 0 || (col == 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7),
                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => (row == 2 || row == 5) && (col == 3 || col == 4),
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    unsafe { 
        asm!("cli");
        init_idt();
        init_pic_pit();
        asm!("sti");
    }

    let total_pixels = height * stride;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1; 
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let w_isize = width as isize;
    let h_isize = height as isize;

    let mut wave: usize = 0;

    loop {
        let current_ticks = unsafe { core::ptr::read_volatile(&SYSTEM_TICKS) };

        let mut status: u8;
        let mut scancode: u8 = 0;
        unsafe {
            asm!("in al, 0x64", out("al") status);
            if (status & 1) == 1 { asm!("in al, 0x60", out("al") scancode); }
        }

        if current_ticks > 5000 || (scancode != 0 && scancode < 0x80) {
            for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00FFFFFF); } }
            for _ in 0..5_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
            
            let app_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, *mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *const usize) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(app_ptr) };
            app_entry(fb_ptr, bb_ptr, width, height, stride, unsafe { &SYSTEM_TICKS as *const usize });
        }

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00000000); } }

        let phase = (wave / 3) % 100;
        let pulse = if phase < 50 { phase as isize } else { (100 - phase) as isize };
        let radius = 100 + (pulse / 2);
        
        for y in 0..h_isize {
            for x in 0..w_isize {
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let offset = (y as usize) * stride + (x as usize);
                    let color = 0xFF000000 | (((pulse * 3) as u32) << 16) | (140 << 8) | 255;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), color); }
                }
            }
        }

        let msg_core = [b'M', b'I', b'N', b'D', b' ', b'C', b'O', b'R', b'E'];
        let msg_time = [b'H', b'W', b' ', b'T', b'I', b'M', b'E', b'R', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 50, &msg_core, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 80, &msg_time, 0x0000FF00);
        
        let mut t_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let t_len = usize_to_str(current_ticks, &mut t_buf);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 150, 80, &t_buf[0..t_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        wave += 1;
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }

$OBJCOPY -O binary -j .text -j .rodata -j .data "$ROOT_DIR/kernel/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/kernel" "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/src/kernel.bin"
$OBJCOPY -O binary -j .text -j .rodata -j .data "$ROOT_DIR/app/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/app" "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/src/app.bin"

cd "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader" && cargo build --release --target x86_64-unknown-uefi

#!/bin/bash
set -e
source "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null || true
export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH"
ROOT_DIR=$(pwd)

echo ">>> Применяем Патч 003: Внедрение int 0x80, разделение счетчиков, фикс вращения..."

cat << 'INNER_KERNEL' > kernel/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]
#![feature(abi_x86_interrupt)]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

unsafe fn outb(port: u16, val: u8) {
    asm!("out dx, al", in("dx") port, in("al") val, options(nomem, nostack));
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
#[repr(C, packed)]
struct IdtEntry {
    offset_low: u16,
    selector: u16,
    ist: u8,
    type_attr: u8,
    offset_mid: u16,
    offset_high: u32,
    zero: u32,
}

#[repr(C, packed)]
struct IdtPtr {
    limit: u16,
    base: u64,
}

#[repr(C)]
pub struct InterruptFrame {
    pub rip: u64,
    pub cs: u64,
    pub rflags: u64,
    pub rsp: u64,
    pub ss: u64,
}

// Структура для передачи параметров системных вызовов
#[repr(C)]
pub struct SyscallMailbox {
    pub syscall_num: usize,
    pub arg1: usize,
    pub result: usize,
}

static mut IDT: [IdtEntry; 256] = [IdtEntry { offset_low: 0, selector: 0, ist: 0, type_attr: 0, offset_mid: 0, offset_high: 0, zero: 0 }; 256];
static mut SYSTEM_TICKS: usize = 0;
static mut MAILBOX: SyscallMailbox = SyscallMailbox { syscall_num: 0, arg1: 0, result: 0 };

extern "x86-interrupt" fn timer_handler(_frame: &mut InterruptFrame) {
    unsafe {
        SYSTEM_TICKS += 1;
        outb(0x20, 0x20); // EOI
    }
}

// Обработчик системных вызовов int 0x80
extern "x86-interrupt" fn syscall_handler(_frame: &mut InterruptFrame) {
    unsafe {
        if MAILBOX.syscall_num == 1 {
            // Системный вызов 1: Получить системное время (тики)
            MAILBOX.result = SYSTEM_TICKS;
        }
    }
}

unsafe fn get_cs() -> u16 {
    let mut cs: u16;
    asm!("mov ax, cs", out("ax") cs, options(nomem, nostack));
    cs
}

unsafe fn init_idt() {
    let cs = get_cs(); 
    
    // Вектор 32: Аппаратный таймер
    let timer_addr = timer_handler as u64;
    IDT[32].offset_low = timer_addr as u16;
    IDT[32].selector = cs;
    IDT[32].ist = 0;
    IDT[32].type_attr = 0x8E;
    IDT[32].offset_mid = (timer_addr >> 16) as u16;
    IDT[32].offset_high = (timer_addr >> 32) as u32;

    // Вектор 128 (0x80): Системные вызовы (Syscalls)
    let sys_addr = syscall_handler as u64;
    IDT[128].offset_low = sys_addr as u16;
    IDT[128].selector = cs;
    IDT[128].ist = 0;
    IDT[128].type_attr = 0x8E; 
    IDT[128].offset_mid = (sys_addr >> 16) as u16;
    IDT[128].offset_high = (sys_addr >> 32) as u32;

    let idt_ptr = IdtPtr {
        limit: (core::mem::size_of::<[IdtEntry; 256]>() - 1) as u16,
        base: IDT.as_ptr() as u64,
    };
    asm!("lidt [{}]", in(reg) &idt_ptr);
}

unsafe fn init_pic_pit() {
    outb(0x20, 0x11); outb(0xA0, 0x11);
    outb(0x21, 0x20); outb(0xA1, 0x28);
    outb(0x21, 0x04); outb(0xA1, 0x02);
    outb(0x21, 0x01); outb(0xA1, 0x01);
    outb(0x21, 0xFE); outb(0xA1, 0xFF); 

    let divisor: u32 = 1193182 / 1000;
    outb(0x43, 0x36);
    outb(0x40, (divisor & 0xFF) as u8);
    outb(0x40, ((divisor >> 8) & 0xFF) as u8);
}

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0' => (row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7,
                b'1' => col == 4 || (row == 1 && col == 3) || row == 7,
                b'2' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 3 && row < 7) || row == 7,
                b'3' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 7 && row != 3,
                b'4' => col == 6 || (row == 4) || (col == 1 && row < 4) || (row + col == 6 && col < 6),
                b'5' => row == 0 || (col == 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7),
                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => (row == 2 || row == 5) && (col == 3 || col == 4),
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    unsafe { 
        asm!("cli");
        init_idt();
        init_pic_pit();
        asm!("sti");
    }

    let total_pixels = height * stride;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1; 
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let w_isize = width as isize;
    let h_isize = height as isize;
    let mut wave: usize = 0;

    loop {
        let current_ticks = unsafe { core::ptr::read_volatile(&SYSTEM_TICKS) };
        let mut status: u8;
        let mut scancode: u8 = 0;
        unsafe {
            asm!("in al, 0x64", out("al") status);
            if (status & 1) == 1 { asm!("in al, 0x60", out("al") scancode); }
        }

        if current_ticks > 4000 || (scancode != 0 && scancode < 0x80) {
            for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00FFFFFF); } }
            for _ in 0..5_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
            
            // Передаем приложению указатель на структуру системных вызовов (Mailbox)
            let app_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, *mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *mut SyscallMailbox) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(app_ptr) };
            app_entry(fb_ptr, bb_ptr, width, height, stride, unsafe { &mut MAILBOX as *mut SyscallMailbox });
        }

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00000000); } }

        let phase = (wave / 3) % 100;
        let pulse = if phase < 50 { phase as isize } else { (100 - phase) as isize };
        let radius = 100 + (pulse / 2);
        
        for y in 0..h_isize {
            for x in 0..w_isize {
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let offset = (y as usize) * stride + (x as usize);
                    let color = 0xFF000000 | (((pulse * 3) as u32) << 16) | (140 << 8) | 255;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), color); }
                }
            }
        }

        let msg_core = [b'I', b'N', b'I', b'T', b' ', b'S', b'Y', b'S', b'C', b'A', b'L', b'L', b'S'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 50, &msg_core, 0x00FFFFFF);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        wave += 1;
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_KERNEL

cat << 'INNER_APP' > app/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::panic::PanicInfo;
use core::arch::asm;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SyscallMailbox {
    pub syscall_num: usize,
    pub arg1: usize,
    pub result: usize,
}

// Обертка для вызова прерывания int 0x80
fn get_os_ticks(mb: *mut SyscallMailbox) -> usize {
    unsafe {
        (*mb).syscall_num = 1; // Команда 1: Запросить тики у ядра
        asm!("int 0x80", options(nostack));
        (*mb).result
    }
}

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0' => (row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7,
                b'1' => col == 4 || (row == 1 && col == 3) || row == 7,
                b'2' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 3 && row < 7) || row == 7,
                b'3' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 7 && row != 3,
                b'4' => col == 6 || (row == 4) || (col == 1 && row < 4) || (row + col == 6 && col < 6),
                b'5' => row == 0 || (col == 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7),
                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => (row == 2 || row == 5) && (col == 3 || col == 4),
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, mb_ptr: *mut SyscallMailbox) -> ! {
    let sin_table: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];
    
    let mut frame_counter: usize = 0; // Счетчик 1: Кадры (Отрисовка)
    let mut work_counter: usize = 0;  // Счетчик 2: Работа (Параллельная задача)
    
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    loop {
        // --- Параллельная вычислительная задача ---
        // Этот цикл имитирует полезную работу между кадрами
        for _ in 0..1000 {
            work_counter += 1;
            unsafe { core::ptr::read_volatile(&work_counter); } // Запрет оптимизации
        }

        frame_counter += 1;

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 120;
        
        // --- МЕДЛЕННОЕ ВРАЩЕНИЕ ---
        // Делитель 30 делает поворот очень плавным
        let mut t_temp = frame_counter / 30; 
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = sin_table[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = sin_table[cos_angle];

        for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
            for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), 0xFF00FF00); }
                }
            }
        }

        let app_msg = [b'U', b'S', b'E', b'R', b'S', b'P', b'A', b'C', b'E'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, &app_msg, 0x0000FF00);

        // 1. Счетчик итераций (кадры)
        let mut f_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let f_len = usize_to_str(frame_counter, &mut f_buf);
        let f_msg = [b'F', b'R', b'A', b'M', b'E', b'S', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, &f_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 70, &f_buf[0..f_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        // 2. Счетчик имитации параллельной задачи
        let mut w_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let w_len = usize_to_str(work_counter, &mut w_buf);
        let w_msg = [b'W', b'O', b'R', b'K', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 100, &w_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 100, &w_buf[0..w_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        // 3. Вызов ядра через программное прерывание int 0x80
        let os_time = get_os_ticks(mb_ptr);
        let mut os_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let os_len = usize_to_str(os_time, &mut os_buf);
        let os_msg = [b'O', b'S', b' ', b'M', b'S', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 130, &os_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 130, &os_buf[0..os_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        // Быстрый рендер без задержек (nop)
        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
    }
}

#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::panic::PanicInfo;
use core::arch::asm;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SyscallMailbox {
    pub syscall_num: usize,
    pub arg1: usize,
    pub result: usize,
}

fn get_os_ticks(mb: *mut SyscallMailbox) -> usize {
    unsafe {
        (*mb).syscall_num = 1; 
        asm!("int 0x80", options(nostack));
        (*mb).result
    }

// --- ПЛАНИРОВЩИК И МНОГОЗАДАЧНОСТЬ ---
static mut MAIN_SP: u64 = 0;
static mut THREAD_SP: u64 = 0;
static mut WORK_COUNTER: usize = 0;

#[repr(align(16))]
struct ThreadStack {
    data: [u64; 1024],
}
static mut THREAD_STACK: ThreadStack = ThreadStack { data: [0; 1024] };

// ИСПРАВЛЕНИЕ: Новый синтаксис Rust для naked-функций
#[unsafe(naked)]
extern "sysv64" fn yield_task(_old_sp: *mut u64, _new_sp: u64) {
    unsafe {
            "push rbx",
            "push rbp",
            "push r12",
            "push r13",
            "push r14",
            "push r15",
            "mov [rdi], rsp",
            "mov rsp, rsi",
            "pop r15",
            "pop r14",
            "pop r13",
            "pop r12",
            "pop rbp",
            "pop rbx",
            "ret",
        );
    }
}

fn background_task() {
    loop {
        for _ in 0..500 {
            unsafe { 
                WORK_COUNTER += 1; 
                core::ptr::read_volatile(&WORK_COUNTER);
            }
        }
        unsafe { yield_task(&mut THREAD_SP, MAIN_SP); }
    }
}

unsafe fn init_thread() {
    let stack_ptr = THREAD_STACK.data.as_mut_ptr().add(1024);
    let mut sp = stack_ptr as u64;
    
    sp -= 8;
    *(sp as *mut u64) = background_task as u64;
    
    for _ in 0..6 {
        sp -= 8;
        *(sp as *mut u64) = 0;
    }
    
    THREAD_SP = sp;
}

                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => (row == 2 || row == 5) && (col == 3 || col == 4),
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }

    fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
        let mut cx = start_x;
        for &b in text {
            let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
            draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
            cx += 10;
        }
    }

    fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
        if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
        let mut idx = 0;
        let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
        while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
            temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
            val /= 10;
            idx += 1;
        }
        for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
        idx
    }

    #[no_mangle]
    #[link_section = ".text._start"]
    pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, mb_ptr: *mut SyscallMailbox) -> ! {
        let sin_table: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];
        
        unsafe { init_thread(); }
        
        let mut frame_counter: usize = 0; 
        let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
        let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
        let total_pixels = height * stride;

        loop {
            // Передаем управление фоновому потоку
            unsafe { yield_task(&mut MAIN_SP, THREAD_SP); }
            
            frame_counter += 1;

            for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

            let size: isize = 120;
            
            let mut t_temp = frame_counter / 30; 
            while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
            let angle = t_temp;
            
            let sin_a = sin_table[angle];
            let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
            if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
            let cos_a = sin_table[cos_angle];

            for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
                for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                    if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                    let dx = x - cx;
                    let dy = y - cy;
                    
                    let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                    let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                    
                    if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                        let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                        unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), 0xFF00FF00); }
                    }
                }
            }

            let app_msg = [b'U', b'S', b'E', b'R', b'S', b'P', b'A', b'C', b'E'];
            draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, &app_msg, 0x0000FF00);

            let mut f_buf = [0u8; 20];
            let f_len = usize_to_str(frame_counter, &mut f_buf);
            let f_msg = [b'F', b'R', b'A', b'M', b'E', b'S', b':'];
            draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, &f_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
            draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 70, &f_buf[0..f_len], 0x00FFFF00);

            let current_work = unsafe { core::ptr::read_volatile(&WORK_COUNTER) };
            let mut w_buf = [0u8; 20];
            let w_len = usize_to_str(current_work, &mut w_buf);
            let w_msg = [b'W', b'O', b'R', b'K', b':'];
            draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 100, &w_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
            draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 100, &w_buf[0..w_len], 0x00FFFF00);

            let os_time = get_os_ticks(mb_ptr);
            let mut os_buf = [0u8; 20];
            let os_len = usize_to_str(os_time, &mut os_buf);
            let os_msg = [b'O', b'S', b' ', b'M', b'S', b':'];
            draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 130, &os_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
            draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 130, &os_buf[0..os_len], 0x00FFFF00);

            for i in 0..total_pixels {
                unsafe {
                    let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                    core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    #[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_APP

cd "$ROOT_DIR/app" && cargo build --release
OBJCOPY=$(find $(rustc --print sysroot) -name llvm-objcopy | head -n 1)
$OBJCOPY -O binary -j .text -j .rodata -j .data "$ROOT_DIR/app/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/app" "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/src/app.bin"
cd "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader" && cargo build --release --target x86_64-unknown-uefi
cp "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/target/x86_64-unknown-uefi/release/bootloader.efi" "$ROOT_DIR/usb_root/EFI/BOOT/BOOTX64.EFI"
