#!/bin/bash
set -e
source "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null || true
export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH"
ROOT_DIR=$(pwd)

cat << 'INNER_KERNEL' > kernel/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]
#![feature(abi_x86_interrupt)]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

unsafe fn outb(port: u16, val: u8) {
    asm!("out dx, al", in("dx") port, in("al") val, options(nomem, nostack));
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
#[repr(C, packed)]
struct IdtEntry {
    offset_low: u16,
    selector: u16,
    ist: u8,
    type_attr: u8,
    offset_mid: u16,
    offset_high: u32,
    zero: u32,
}

#[repr(C, packed)]
struct IdtPtr {
    limit: u16,
    base: u64,
}

#[repr(C)]
pub struct InterruptFrame {
    pub rip: u64,
    pub cs: u64,
    pub rflags: u64,
    pub rsp: u64,
    pub ss: u64,
}

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SyscallMailbox {
    pub syscall_num: usize,
    pub arg1: usize,
    pub result: usize,
}

static mut IDT: [IdtEntry; 256] = [IdtEntry { offset_low: 0, selector: 0, ist: 0, type_attr: 0, offset_mid: 0, offset_high: 0, zero: 0 }; 256];
static mut SYSTEM_TICKS: usize = 0;
static mut MAILBOX: SyscallMailbox = SyscallMailbox { syscall_num: 0, arg1: 0, result: 0 };

extern "x86-interrupt" fn timer_handler(_frame: &mut InterruptFrame) {
    unsafe {
        let ptr = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(SYSTEM_TICKS);
        let ticks = core::ptr::read_volatile(ptr);
        core::ptr::write_volatile(ptr, ticks + 1);
        outb(0x20, 0x20); 
    }
}

extern "x86-interrupt" fn syscall_handler(_frame: &mut InterruptFrame) {
    unsafe {
        let mb = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(MAILBOX);
        if (*mb).syscall_num == 1 {
            (*mb).result = core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(SYSTEM_TICKS));
        }
    }
}

unsafe fn get_cs() -> u16 {
    let mut cs: u16;
    asm!("mov ax, cs", out("ax") cs, options(nomem, nostack));
    cs
}

unsafe fn init_idt() {
    let cs = get_cs(); 
    
    let timer_addr = timer_handler as *const () as u64;
    IDT[32].offset_low = timer_addr as u16;
    IDT[32].selector = cs;
    IDT[32].ist = 0;
    IDT[32].type_attr = 0x8E;
    IDT[32].offset_mid = (timer_addr >> 16) as u16;
    IDT[32].offset_high = (timer_addr >> 32) as u32;

    let sys_addr = syscall_handler as *const () as u64;
    IDT[128].offset_low = sys_addr as u16;
    IDT[128].selector = cs;
    IDT[128].ist = 0;
    IDT[128].type_attr = 0x8E; 
    IDT[128].offset_mid = (sys_addr >> 16) as u16;
    IDT[128].offset_high = (sys_addr >> 32) as u32;

    let idt_ptr = IdtPtr {
        limit: (core::mem::size_of::<[IdtEntry; 256]>() - 1) as u16,
        base: core::ptr::addr_of!(IDT) as u64,
    };
    asm!("lidt [{}]", in(reg) &idt_ptr);
}

unsafe fn init_pic_pit() {
    outb(0x20, 0x11); outb(0xA0, 0x11);
    outb(0x21, 0x20); outb(0xA1, 0x28);
    outb(0x21, 0x04); outb(0xA1, 0x02);
    outb(0x21, 0x01); outb(0xA1, 0x01);
    outb(0x21, 0xFE); outb(0xA1, 0xFF); 

    let divisor: u32 = 1193182 / 1000;
    outb(0x43, 0x36);
    outb(0x40, (divisor & 0xFF) as u8);
    outb(0x40, ((divisor >> 8) & 0xFF) as u8);
}

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0' => (row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7,
                b'1' => col == 4 || (row == 1 && col == 3) || row == 7,
                b'2' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 3 && row < 7) || row == 7,
                b'3' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 7 && row != 3,
                b'4' => col == 6 || (row == 4) || (col == 1 && row < 4) || (row + col == 6 && col < 6),
                b'5' => row == 0 || (col == 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7),
                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => (row == 2 || row == 5) && (col == 3 || col == 4),
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    unsafe { 
        asm!("cli");
        init_idt();
        init_pic_pit();
        asm!("sti");
    }

    let total_pixels = height * stride;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1; 
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let w_isize = width as isize;
    let h_isize = height as isize;
    let mut wave: usize = 0;

    loop {
        let current_ticks = unsafe { core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(SYSTEM_TICKS)) };
        let mut status: u8;
        let mut scancode: u8 = 0;
        unsafe {
            asm!("in al, 0x64", out("al") status);
            if (status & 1) == 1 { asm!("in al, 0x60", out("al") scancode); }
        }

        if current_ticks > 4000 || (scancode != 0 && scancode < 0x80) {
            for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00FFFFFF); } }
            for _ in 0..5_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
            
            let app_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, *mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *mut SyscallMailbox) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(app_ptr) };
            app_entry(fb_ptr, bb_ptr, width, height, stride, unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(MAILBOX) });
        }

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00000000); } }

        let phase = (wave / 3) % 100;
        let pulse = if phase < 50 { phase as isize } else { (100 - phase) as isize };
        let radius = 100 + (pulse / 2);
        
        for y in 0..h_isize {
            for x in 0..w_isize {
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let offset = (y as usize) * stride + (x as usize);
                    let color = 0xFF000000 | (((pulse * 3) as u32) << 16) | (140 << 8) | 255;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), color); }
                }
            }
        }

        let msg_core = [b'I', b'N', b'I', b'T', b' ', b'S', b'Y', b'S', b'C', b'A', b'L', b'L', b'S'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 50, &msg_core, 0x00FFFFFF);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        wave += 1;
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_KERNEL

cat << 'INNER_APP' > app/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::panic::PanicInfo;
use core::arch::asm;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SyscallMailbox {
    pub syscall_num: usize,
    pub arg1: usize,
    pub result: usize,
}

fn get_os_ticks(mb: *mut SyscallMailbox) -> usize {
    unsafe {
        (*mb).syscall_num = 1; 
        asm!("int 0x80", options(nostack));
        (*mb).result
    }
}

static mut MAIN_SP: u64 = 0;
static mut THREAD_SP: u64 = 0;
static mut WORK_COUNTER: usize = 0;

#[repr(align(16))]
struct ThreadStack {
    data: [u64; 1024],
}
static mut THREAD_STACK: ThreadStack = ThreadStack { data: [0; 1024] };

#[unsafe(naked)]
extern "sysv64" fn yield_task(_old_sp: *mut u64, _new_sp: u64) {
    unsafe {
        core::arch::naked_asm!(
            "push rbx",
            "push rbp",
            "push r12",
            "push r13",
            "push r14",
            "push r15",
            "mov [rdi], rsp",
            "mov rsp, rsi",
            "pop r15",
            "pop r14",
            "pop r13",
            "pop r12",
            "pop rbp",
            "pop rbx",
            "ret",
        );
    }
}

fn background_task() {
    loop {
        for _ in 0..500 {
            unsafe { 
                let ptr = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(WORK_COUNTER);
                let count = core::ptr::read_volatile(ptr);
                core::ptr::write_volatile(ptr, count + 1); 
            }
        }
        unsafe { yield_task(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(THREAD_SP), core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(MAIN_SP))); }
    }
}

unsafe fn init_thread() {
    let stack_ptr = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(THREAD_STACK.data) as *mut u64;
    let mut sp = stack_ptr.add(1024) as u64;
    
    sp -= 8;
    *(sp as *mut u64) = background_task as *const () as u64;
    
    for _ in 0..6 {
        sp -= 8;
        *(sp as *mut u64) = 0;
    }
    
    core::ptr::write_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(THREAD_SP), sp);
}

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0' => (row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7,
                b'1' => col == 4 || (row == 1 && col == 3) || row == 7,
                b'2' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 3 && row < 7) || row == 7,
                b'3' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 7 && row != 3,
                b'4' => col == 6 || (row == 4) || (col == 1 && row < 4) || (row + col == 6 && col < 6),
                b'5' => row == 0 || (col == 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7),
                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => (row == 2 || row == 5) && (col == 3 || col == 4),
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, mb_ptr: *mut SyscallMailbox) -> ! {
    let sin_table: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];
    
    unsafe { init_thread(); }
    
    let mut frame_counter: usize = 0; 
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    loop {
        unsafe { yield_task(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(MAIN_SP), core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(THREAD_SP))); }
        
        frame_counter += 1;

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 120;
        
        let mut t_temp = frame_counter / 30; 
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = sin_table[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = sin_table[cos_angle];

        for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
            for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), 0xFF00FF00); }
                }
            }
        }

        let app_msg = [b'U', b'S', b'E', b'R', b'S', b'P', b'A', b'C', b'E'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, &app_msg, 0x0000FF00);

        let mut f_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let f_len = usize_to_str(frame_counter, &mut f_buf);
        let f_msg = [b'F', b'R', b'A', b'M', b'E', b'S', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, &f_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 70, &f_buf[0..f_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        let current_work = unsafe { core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(WORK_COUNTER)) };
        let mut w_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let w_len = usize_to_str(current_work, &mut w_buf);
        let w_msg = [b'W', b'O', b'R', b'K', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 100, &w_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 100, &w_buf[0..w_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        let os_time = get_os_ticks(mb_ptr);
        let mut os_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let os_len = usize_to_str(os_time, &mut os_buf);
        let os_msg = [b'O', b'S', b' ', b'M', b'S', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 130, &os_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 130, &os_buf[0..os_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }

#!/bin/bash
set -e
source "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null || true
export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH"
ROOT_DIR=$(pwd)

echo ">>> Применяем Патч 006: Аппаратные прерывания клавиатуры и интерактивное API..."

cat << 'INNER_KERNEL' > kernel/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]
#![feature(abi_x86_interrupt)]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

unsafe fn outb(port: u16, val: u8) {
    asm!("out dx, al", in("dx") port, in("al") val, options(nomem, nostack));
}

unsafe fn inb(port: u16) -> u8 {
    let mut val: u8;
    asm!("in al, dx", out("al") val, in("dx") port, options(nomem, nostack));
    val
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
#[repr(C, packed)]
struct IdtEntry {
    offset_low: u16,
    selector: u16,
    ist: u8,
    type_attr: u8,
    offset_mid: u16,
    offset_high: u32,
    zero: u32,
}

#[repr(C, packed)]
struct IdtPtr {
    limit: u16,
    base: u64,
}

#[repr(C)]
pub struct InterruptFrame {
    pub rip: u64,
    pub cs: u64,
    pub rflags: u64,
    pub rsp: u64,
    pub ss: u64,
}

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SyscallMailbox {
    pub syscall_num: usize,
    pub arg1: usize,
    pub result: usize,
}

static mut IDT: [IdtEntry; 256] = [IdtEntry { offset_low: 0, selector: 0, ist: 0, type_attr: 0, offset_mid: 0, offset_high: 0, zero: 0 }; 256];
static mut SYSTEM_TICKS: usize = 0;
static mut LAST_KEY: u8 = 0;
static mut MAILBOX: SyscallMailbox = SyscallMailbox { syscall_num: 0, arg1: 0, result: 0 };

extern "x86-interrupt" fn timer_handler(_frame: &mut InterruptFrame) {
    unsafe {
        let ptr = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(SYSTEM_TICKS);
        let ticks = core::ptr::read_volatile(ptr);
        core::ptr::write_volatile(ptr, ticks + 1);
        outb(0x20, 0x20); // Аппаратное подтверждение (EOI)
    }
}

// Новый обработчик: Аппаратное прерывание клавиатуры (PS/2)
extern "x86-interrupt" fn keyboard_handler(_frame: &mut InterruptFrame) {
    unsafe {
        let scancode = inb(0x60); // Читаем сканкод нажатой клавиши напрямую из порта
        core::ptr::write_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(LAST_KEY), scancode);
        outb(0x20, 0x20); // EOI
    }
}

extern "x86-interrupt" fn syscall_handler(_frame: &mut InterruptFrame) {
    unsafe {
        let mb = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(MAILBOX);
        if (*mb).syscall_num == 1 {
            (*mb).result = core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(SYSTEM_TICKS));
        } else if (*mb).syscall_num == 2 {
            // Системный вызов 2: Получить последнюю нажатую клавишу
            let key = core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(LAST_KEY));
            (*mb).result = key as usize;
            core::ptr::write_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(LAST_KEY), 0); // Очищаем буфер после чтения
        }
    }
}

unsafe fn get_cs() -> u16 {
    let mut cs: u16;
    asm!("mov ax, cs", out("ax") cs, options(nomem, nostack));
    cs
}

unsafe fn init_idt() {
    let cs = get_cs(); 
    
    // Вектор 32: Таймер
    let timer_addr = timer_handler as *const () as u64;
    IDT[32].offset_low = timer_addr as u16;
    IDT[32].selector = cs;
    IDT[32].ist = 0;
    IDT[32].type_attr = 0x8E;
    IDT[32].offset_mid = (timer_addr >> 16) as u16;
    IDT[32].offset_high = (timer_addr >> 32) as u32;

    // Вектор 33: Клавиатура
    let kbd_addr = keyboard_handler as *const () as u64;
    IDT[33].offset_low = kbd_addr as u16;
    IDT[33].selector = cs;
    IDT[33].ist = 0;
    IDT[33].type_attr = 0x8E;
    IDT[33].offset_mid = (kbd_addr >> 16) as u16;
    IDT[33].offset_high = (kbd_addr >> 32) as u32;

    // Вектор 128: Системные вызовы (Syscalls)
    let sys_addr = syscall_handler as *const () as u64;
    IDT[128].offset_low = sys_addr as u16;
    IDT[128].selector = cs;
    IDT[128].ist = 0;
    IDT[128].type_attr = 0x8E; 
    IDT[128].offset_mid = (sys_addr >> 16) as u16;
    IDT[128].offset_high = (sys_addr >> 32) as u32;

    let idt_ptr = IdtPtr {
        limit: (core::mem::size_of::<[IdtEntry; 256]>() - 1) as u16,
        base: core::ptr::addr_of!(IDT) as u64,
    };
    asm!("lidt [{}]", in(reg) &idt_ptr);
}

unsafe fn init_pic_pit() {
    outb(0x20, 0x11); outb(0xA0, 0x11);
    outb(0x21, 0x20); outb(0xA1, 0x28);
    outb(0x21, 0x04); outb(0xA1, 0x02);
    outb(0x21, 0x01); outb(0xA1, 0x01);
    
    // 0xFC (11111100): Снимаем маску с IRQ0 (Таймер) и IRQ1 (Клавиатура)
    outb(0x21, 0xFC); outb(0xA1, 0xFF); 

    let divisor: u32 = 1193182 / 1000;
    outb(0x43, 0x36);
    outb(0x40, (divisor & 0xFF) as u8);
    outb(0x40, ((divisor >> 8) & 0xFF) as u8);
}

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0' => (row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7,
                b'1' => col == 4 || (row == 1 && col == 3) || row == 7,
                b'2' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 3 && row < 7) || row == 7,
                b'3' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 7 && row != 3,
                b'4' => col == 6 || (row == 4) || (col == 1 && row < 4) || (row + col == 6 && col < 6),
                b'5' => row == 0 || (col == 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7),
                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => (row == 2 || row == 5) && (col == 3 || col == 4),
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    unsafe { 
        asm!("cli");
        init_idt();
        init_pic_pit();
        asm!("sti");
    }

    let total_pixels = height * stride;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1; 
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let w_isize = width as isize;
    let h_isize = height as isize;
    let mut wave: usize = 0;

    loop {
        let current_ticks = unsafe { core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(SYSTEM_TICKS)) };

        // Ядро больше не опрашивает порты в цикле, переход чисто по аппаратному таймеру (3 секунды)
        if current_ticks > 3000 {
            for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00FFFFFF); } }
            for _ in 0..5_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
            
            let app_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, *mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *mut SyscallMailbox) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(app_ptr) };
            app_entry(fb_ptr, bb_ptr, width, height, stride, unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(MAILBOX) });
        }

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00000000); } }

        let phase = (wave / 3) % 100;
        let pulse = if phase < 50 { phase as isize } else { (100 - phase) as isize };
        let radius = 100 + (pulse / 2);
        
        for y in 0..h_isize {
            for x in 0..w_isize {
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let offset = (y as usize) * stride + (x as usize);
                    let color = 0xFF000000 | (((pulse * 3) as u32) << 16) | (140 << 8) | 255;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), color); }
                }
            }
        }

        let msg_core = [b'M', b'I', b'N', b'D', b' ', b'C', b'O', b'R', b'E'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 50, &msg_core, 0x00FFFFFF);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
        wave += 1;
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }

cat << 'INNER_APP' > app/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

use core::panic::PanicInfo;
use core::arch::asm;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SyscallMailbox {
    pub syscall_num: usize,
    pub arg1: usize,
    pub result: usize,
}

fn get_os_ticks(mb: *mut SyscallMailbox) -> usize {
    unsafe {
        (*mb).syscall_num = 1; 
        asm!("int 0x80", options(nostack));
        (*mb).result
    }
}

fn get_os_key(mb: *mut SyscallMailbox) -> u8 {
    unsafe {
        (*mb).syscall_num = 2; 
        asm!("int 0x80", options(nostack));
        (*mb).result as u8
    }
}

static mut MAIN_SP: u64 = 0;
static mut THREAD_SP: u64 = 0;
static mut WORK_COUNTER: usize = 0;

#[repr(align(16))]
struct ThreadStack {
    data: [u64; 1024],
}
static mut THREAD_STACK: ThreadStack = ThreadStack { data: [0; 1024] };

#[unsafe(naked)]
extern "sysv64" fn yield_task(_old_sp: *mut u64, _new_sp: u64) {
    unsafe {
        core::arch::naked_asm!(
            "push rbx",
            "push rbp",
            "push r12",
            "push r13",
            "push r14",
            "push r15",
            "mov [rdi], rsp",
            "mov rsp, rsi",
            "pop r15",
            "pop r14",
            "pop r13",
            "pop r12",
            "pop rbp",
            "pop rbx",
            "ret",
        );
    }
}

fn background_task() {
    loop {
        for _ in 0..500 {
            unsafe { 
                let ptr = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(WORK_COUNTER);
                let count = core::ptr::read_volatile(ptr);
                core::ptr::write_volatile(ptr, count + 1); 
            }
        }
        unsafe { yield_task(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(THREAD_SP), core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(MAIN_SP))); }
    }
}

unsafe fn init_thread() {
    let stack_ptr = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(THREAD_STACK.data) as *mut u64;
    let mut sp = stack_ptr.add(1024) as u64;
    
    sp -= 8;
    *(sp as *mut u64) = background_task as *const () as u64;
    
    for _ in 0..6 {
        sp -= 8;
        *(sp as *mut u64) = 0;
    }
    
    core::ptr::write_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(THREAD_SP), sp);
}

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0' => (row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7,
                b'1' => col == 4 || (row == 1 && col == 3) || row == 7,
                b'2' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 3 && row < 7) || row == 7,
                b'3' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 7 && row != 3,
                b'4' => col == 6 || (row == 4) || (col == 1 && row < 4) || (row + col == 6 && col < 6),
                b'5' => row == 0 || (col == 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7),
                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => (row == 2 || row == 5) && (col == 3 || col == 4),
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, mb_ptr: *mut SyscallMailbox) -> ! {
    let sin_table: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];
    
    unsafe { init_thread(); }
    
    let mut frame_counter: usize = 0; 
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    let mut color_theme: u32 = 0x0000FF00; // Зеленый по умолчанию
    let mut last_seen_key: u8 = 0;

    loop {
        unsafe { yield_task(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(MAIN_SP), core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(THREAD_SP))); }
        
        // Обработка пользовательского ввода
        let key = get_os_key(mb_ptr);
        if key != 0 {
            // Игнорируем события отжатия клавиши (с кодом > 0x80) для цветовых тем
            if key < 0x80 {
                last_seen_key = key;
                if key == 0x39 { // Space
                    color_theme = 0x00FF0000; // Красный
                } else if key == 0x1C { // Enter
                    color_theme = 0x000000FF; // Синий
                } else if key == 0x01 { // Esc
                    color_theme = 0x0000FF00; // Зеленый
                }
            }
        }

        frame_counter += 1;

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 120;
        
        let mut t_temp = frame_counter / 30; 
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = sin_table[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = sin_table[cos_angle];

        for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
            for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), color_theme); } // Применяем текущую тему
                }
            }
        }

        let app_msg = [b'U', b'S', b'E', b'R', b'S', b'P', b'A', b'C', b'E'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, &app_msg, color_theme);

        let mut f_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let f_len = usize_to_str(frame_counter, &mut f_buf);
        let f_msg = [b'F', b'R', b'A', b'M', b'E', b'S', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, &f_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 70, &f_buf[0..f_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        let current_work = unsafe { core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(WORK_COUNTER)) };
        let mut w_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let w_len = usize_to_str(current_work, &mut w_buf);
        let w_msg = [b'W', b'O', b'R', b'K', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 100, &w_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 100, &w_buf[0..w_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        let os_time = get_os_ticks(mb_ptr);
        let mut os_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let os_len = usize_to_str(os_time, &mut os_buf);
        let os_msg = [b'O', b'S', b' ', b'M', b'S', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 130, &os_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 130, &os_buf[0..os_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        // Вывод последней нажатой клавиши (Сканкод)
        let mut k_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let k_len = usize_to_str(last_seen_key as usize, &mut k_buf);
        let k_msg = [b'K', b'E', b'Y', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 160, &k_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 160, &k_buf[0..k_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }

#!/bin/bash
set -e
source "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null || true
export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH"
ROOT_DIR=$(pwd)

echo ">>> Применяем Патч 004: Кооперативный Планировщик Потоков и Контекстное Переключение..."

cat << 'INNER_APP' > app/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]
#![feature(naked_functions)] // Разрешаем чистые ассемблерные функции для переключения контекста

use core::panic::PanicInfo;
use core::arch::asm;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SyscallMailbox {
    pub syscall_num: usize,
    pub arg1: usize,
    pub result: usize,
}

fn get_os_ticks(mb: *mut SyscallMailbox) -> usize {
    unsafe {
        (*mb).syscall_num = 1; 
        asm!("int 0x80", options(nostack));
        (*mb).result
    }
}

// --- ПЛАНИРОВЩИК И МНОГОЗАДАЧНОСТЬ ---
static mut MAIN_SP: u64 = 0;
static mut THREAD_SP: u64 = 0;
static mut WORK_COUNTER: usize = 0;

// Выделяем изолированный выровненный стек для второго потока
#[repr(align(16))]
struct ThreadStack {
    data: [u64; 1024],
}
static mut THREAD_STACK: ThreadStack = ThreadStack { data: [0; 1024] };

// Аппаратное переключение контекста (Context Switch)
#[naked]
extern "sysv64" fn yield_task(_old_sp: *mut u64, _new_sp: u64) {
    unsafe {
        asm!(
            "push rbx",
            "push rbp",
            "push r12",
            "push r13",
            "push r14",
            "push r15",
            "mov [rdi], rsp",  // Сохраняем текущий указатель стека в old_sp
            "mov rsp, rsi",    // Загружаем новый указатель стека из new_sp
            "pop r15",
            "pop r14",
            "pop r13",
            "pop r12",
            "pop rbp",
            "pop rbx",
            "ret",             // Прыжок по адресу возврата с НОВОГО стека!
            options(noreturn)
        );
    }
}

// Это независимый поток. У него своя жизнь и свой стек.
fn background_task() {
    loop {
        // Выполняем полезную работу
        for _ in 0..500 {
            unsafe { 
                WORK_COUNTER += 1; 
                core::ptr::read_volatile(&WORK_COUNTER);
            }
        }
        // Отдаем процессорное время обратно главному потоку отрисовки
        unsafe { yield_task(&mut THREAD_SP, MAIN_SP); }
    }
}

// Подготовка стека перед первым запуском потока
unsafe fn init_thread() {
    let stack_ptr = THREAD_STACK.data.as_mut_ptr().add(1024);
    let mut sp = stack_ptr as u64;
    
    // Подкладываем адрес функции на вершину стека (для команды ret)
    sp -= 8;
    *(sp as *mut u64) = background_task as u64;
    
    // Имитируем 6 пустых callee-saved регистров
    for _ in 0..6 {
        sp -= 8;
        *(sp as *mut u64) = 0;
    }
    
    THREAD_SP = sp;
}
// -------------------------------------

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0' => (row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7,
                b'1' => col == 4 || (row == 1 && col == 3) || row == 7,
                b'2' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 3 && row < 7) || row == 7,
                b'3' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 7 && row != 3,
                b'4' => col == 6 || (row == 4) || (col == 1 && row < 4) || (row + col == 6 && col < 6),
                b'5' => row == 0 || (col == 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7),
                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => (row == 2 || row == 5) && (col == 3 || col == 4),
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &[u8], color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for &b in text {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

fn usize_to_str(mut val: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if val == 0 { buf[0] = b'0'; return 1; }
    let mut idx = 0;
    let mut temp = [0u8; 20];
    while val > 0 && idx < 20 {
        temp[idx] = b'0' + (val % 10) as u8;
        val /= 10;
        idx += 1;
    }
    for i in 0..idx { buf[i] = temp[idx - 1 - i]; }
    idx
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, mb_ptr: *mut SyscallMailbox) -> ! {
    let sin_table: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];
    
    unsafe { init_thread(); } // Инициализируем поток планировщика
    
    let mut frame_counter: usize = 0; 
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    loop {
        // 1. Уступаем управление (Прыгаем в стек фонового потока)
        unsafe { yield_task(&mut MAIN_SP, THREAD_SP); }
        
        frame_counter += 1;

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 120;
        
        // 30 - делитель для плавного и медленного вращения
        let mut t_temp = frame_counter / 30; 
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = sin_table[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = sin_table[cos_angle];

        for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
            for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx;
                let dy = y - cy;
                
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), 0xFF00FF00); }
                }
            }
        }

        let app_msg = [b'U', b'S', b'E', b'R', b'S', b'P', b'A', b'C', b'E'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, &app_msg, 0x0000FF00);

        // Главный поток рисует кадры
        let mut f_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let f_len = usize_to_str(frame_counter, &mut f_buf);
        let f_msg = [b'F', b'R', b'A', b'M', b'E', b'S', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, &f_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 70, &f_buf[0..f_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        // Читаем глобальный счетчик, измененный Фоновым потоком
        let current_work = unsafe { core::ptr::read_volatile(&WORK_COUNTER) };
        let mut w_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let w_len = usize_to_str(current_work, &mut w_buf);
        let w_msg = [b'W', b'O', b'R', b'K', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 100, &w_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 100, &w_buf[0..w_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        // Обращаемся к ядру за системным временем
        let os_time = get_os_ticks(mb_ptr);
        let mut os_buf = [0u8; 20];
        let os_len = usize_to_str(os_time, &mut os_buf);
        let os_msg = [b'O', b'S', b' ', b'M', b'S', b':'];
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 130, &os_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 120, 130, &os_buf[0..os_len], 0x00FFFF00);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_APP

cd "$ROOT_DIR/app" && cargo build --release
OBJCOPY=$(find $(rustc --print sysroot) -name llvm-objcopy | head -n 1)
$OBJCOPY -O binary -j .text -j .rodata -j .data "$ROOT_DIR/app/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/app" "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/src/app.bin"
cd "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader" && cargo build --release --target x86_64-unknown-uefi

=== file: patches_seq/patch_008_preemptive.sh
=== file patch_008_preemptive.sh content:
#!/bin/bash
set -e
source "$HOME/.cargo/env" 2>/dev/null || true
export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH"
ROOT_DIR=$(pwd)

echo ">>> Применяем Патч 008: Вытесняющая Многозадачность (Preemptive Scheduler) в Ядре..."

cat << 'INNER_KERNEL' > kernel/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]
#![feature(abi_x86_interrupt)]

use core::arch::asm;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;

unsafe fn outb(port: u16, val: u8) {
    asm!("out dx, al", in("dx") port, in("al") val, options(nomem, nostack));
}

unsafe fn inb(port: u16) -> u8 {
    let mut val: u8;
    asm!("in al, dx", out("al") val, in("dx") port, options(nomem, nostack));
    val
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
#[repr(C, packed)]
struct IdtEntry {
    offset_low: u16, selector: u16, ist: u8, type_attr: u8,
    offset_mid: u16, offset_high: u32, zero: u32,
}

#[repr(C, packed)]
struct IdtPtr { limit: u16, base: u64 }

#[repr(C)]
pub struct InterruptFrame {
    pub rip: u64, pub cs: u64, pub rflags: u64, pub rsp: u64, pub ss: u64,
}

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SyscallMailbox {
    pub syscall_num: usize,
    pub arg1: usize,
    pub arg2: usize, // Добавлен второй аргумент для указателя стека
    pub result: usize,
}

// --- СТРУКТУРЫ ПЛАНИРОВЩИКА ОС ---
#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
struct Task {
    rsp: u64,
    active: bool,
}
static mut TASKS: [Task; 4] = [Task { rsp: 0, active: false }; 4];
static mut CURRENT_TASK: usize = 0;
static mut MULTITASKING_ENABLED: bool = false;

static mut IDT: [IdtEntry; 256] = [IdtEntry { offset_low: 0, selector: 0, ist: 0, type_attr: 0, offset_mid: 0, offset_high: 0, zero: 0 }; 256];
static mut SYSTEM_TICKS: usize = 0;
static mut LAST_KEY: u8 = 0;
static mut MAILBOX: SyscallMailbox = SyscallMailbox { syscall_num: 0, arg1: 0, arg2: 0, result: 0 };

// --- АССЕМБЛЕРНАЯ ОБЕРТКА ТАЙМЕРА ДЛЯ CONTEXT SWITCH ---
#[unsafe(naked)]
extern "C" fn timer_interrupt_wrapper() {
    unsafe {
        core::arch::naked_asm!(
            "push r15", "push r14", "push r13", "push r12", "push r11", "push r10", "push r9", "push r8",
            "push rdi", "push rsi", "push rbp", "push rbx", "push rdx", "push rcx", "push rax",
            "mov rdi, rsp", // rdi = текущий rsp (передаем как аргумент в timer_tick)
            "call timer_tick",
            "mov rsp, rax", // rax = rsp следующей задачи (возвращается из timer_tick)
            "pop rax", "pop rcx", "pop rdx", "pop rbx", "pop rbp", "pop rsi", "pop rdi",
            "pop r8", "pop r9", "pop r10", "pop r11", "pop r12", "pop r13", "pop r14", "pop r15",
            "iretq"
        );
    }
}

// Главная логика вытесняющего шедулера (Round-Robin)
#[no_mangle]
pub extern "sysv64" fn timer_tick(rsp: u64) -> u64 {
    unsafe {
        let ptr = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(SYSTEM_TICKS);
        core::ptr::write_volatile(ptr, core::ptr::read_volatile(ptr) + 1);
        outb(0x20, 0x20); // EOI

        if core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(MULTITASKING_ENABLED)) {
            let current = core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(CURRENT_TASK));
            TASKS[current].rsp = rsp; // Сохраняем стек прерванной задачи
            
            let mut next = current;
            loop {
                next = (next + 1) % 4;
                if TASKS[next].active { break; } // Ищем следующую живую задачу
            }
            
            core::ptr::write_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(CURRENT_TASK), next);
            return TASKS[next].rsp; // Возвращаем стек новой задачи
        }
        rsp
    }
}

extern "x86-interrupt" fn keyboard_handler(_frame: &mut InterruptFrame) {
    unsafe {
        let scancode = inb(0x60);
        core::ptr::write_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(LAST_KEY), scancode);
        outb(0x20, 0x20);
    }
}

extern "x86-interrupt" fn syscall_handler(_frame: &mut InterruptFrame) {
    unsafe {
        let mb = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(MAILBOX);
        let sys_num = (*mb).syscall_num;
        if sys_num == 1 {
            (*mb).result = core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(SYSTEM_TICKS));
        } else if sys_num == 2 {
            (*mb).result = core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(LAST_KEY)) as usize;
            core::ptr::write_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(LAST_KEY), 0);
        } else if sys_num == 3 {
            // Системный вызов 3: Спавн нового потока ОС
            let entry = (*mb).arg1 as u64;
            let stack_top = (*mb).arg2 as u64;
            let cs = get_cs() as u64;
            let ss = get_ss() as u64;
            
            // Настраиваем фрейм для инструкции iretq новой задачи
            let mut sp = stack_top as *mut u64;
            sp = sp.sub(1); *sp = ss;
            sp = sp.sub(1); *sp = stack_top;
            sp = sp.sub(1); *sp = 0x202; // RFLAGS (Interrupts enabled)
            sp = sp.sub(1); *sp = cs;
            sp = sp.sub(1); *sp = entry; // RIP
            
            // Заглушки для 15 регистров общего назначения
            for _ in 0..15 { sp = sp.sub(1); *sp = 0; }
            
            // Регистрируем задачу в планировщике
            for i in 1..4 {
                if !TASKS[i].active {
                    TASKS[i].rsp = sp as u64;
                    TASKS[i].active = true;
                    (*mb).result = i;
                    break;
                }
            }
        } else if sys_num == 4 {
            // Системный вызов 4: Включить аппаратное вытеснение
            TASKS[0].active = true; // Нулевая задача - это текущий (главный) поток
            core::ptr::write_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(MULTITASKING_ENABLED), true);
        }
    }
}

unsafe fn get_cs() -> u16 { let mut cs: u16; asm!("mov ax, cs", out("ax") cs, options(nomem, nostack)); cs }
unsafe fn get_ss() -> u16 { let mut ss: u16; asm!("mov ax, ss", out("ax") ss, options(nomem, nostack)); ss }

unsafe fn init_idt() {
    let cs = get_cs(); 
    
    // Вектор 32: Аппаратный таймер теперь указывает на asm-обертку!
    let timer_addr = timer_interrupt_wrapper as *const () as u64;
    IDT[32].offset_low = timer_addr as u16;
    IDT[32].selector = cs;
    IDT[32].ist = 0;
    IDT[32].type_attr = 0x8E;
    IDT[32].offset_mid = (timer_addr >> 16) as u16;
    IDT[32].offset_high = (timer_addr >> 32) as u32;

    let kbd_addr = keyboard_handler as *const () as u64;
    IDT[33].offset_low = kbd_addr as u16;
    IDT[33].selector = cs;
    IDT[33].ist = 0;
    IDT[33].type_attr = 0x8E;
    IDT[33].offset_mid = (kbd_addr >> 16) as u16;
    IDT[33].offset_high = (kbd_addr >> 32) as u32;

    let sys_addr = syscall_handler as *const () as u64;
    IDT[128].offset_low = sys_addr as u16;
    IDT[128].selector = cs;
    IDT[128].ist = 0;
    IDT[128].type_attr = 0x8E; 
    IDT[128].offset_mid = (sys_addr >> 16) as u16;
    IDT[128].offset_high = (sys_addr >> 32) as u32;

    let idt_ptr = IdtPtr { limit: (core::mem::size_of::<[IdtEntry; 256]>() - 1) as u16, base: core::ptr::addr_of!(IDT) as u64 };
    asm!("lidt [{}]", in(reg) &idt_ptr);
}

unsafe fn init_pic_pit() {
    outb(0x20, 0x11); outb(0xA0, 0x11);
    outb(0x21, 0x20); outb(0xA1, 0x28);
    outb(0x21, 0x04); outb(0xA1, 0x02);
    outb(0x21, 0x01); outb(0xA1, 0x01);
    outb(0x21, 0xFC); outb(0xA1, 0xFF); 

    let divisor: u32 = 1193182 / 1000;
    outb(0x43, 0x36);
    outb(0x40, (divisor & 0xFF) as u8);
    outb(0x40, ((divisor >> 8) & 0xFF) as u8);
}

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &str, color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for b in text.bytes() {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, app_ptr: *const u8) -> ! {
    unsafe { asm!("cli"); init_idt(); init_pic_pit(); asm!("sti"); }
    let total_pixels = height * stride;
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1; 
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let w_isize = width as isize;
    let h_isize = height as isize;
    let mut wave: usize = 0;

    loop {
        let current_ticks = unsafe { core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(SYSTEM_TICKS)) };
        let mut scancode: u8 = 0;
        unsafe { let mut status: u8; asm!("in al, 0x64", out("al") status); if (status & 1) == 1 { asm!("in al, 0x60", out("al") scancode); } }

        if current_ticks > 3000 || (scancode != 0 && scancode < 0x80) {
            for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00000000); } }
            for _ in 0..5_000_000 { unsafe { asm!("nop"); } }
            let app_entry: extern "sysv64" fn(*mut u32, *mut u32, usize, usize, usize, *mut SyscallMailbox) -> ! = unsafe { core::mem::transmute(app_ptr) };
            app_entry(fb_ptr, bb_ptr, width, height, stride, unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(MAILBOX) });
        }

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00000000); } }
        let phase = (wave / 3) % 100;
        let pulse = if phase < 50 { phase as isize } else { (100 - phase) as isize };
        let radius = 100 + (pulse / 2);
        
        for y in 0..h_isize {
            for x in 0..w_isize {
                let dx = x - cx; let dy = y - cy;
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius {
                    let offset = (y as usize) * stride + (x as usize);
                    let color = 0xFF000000 | (((pulse * 3) as u32) << 16) | (140 << 8) | 255;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), color); }
                }
            }
        }
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 50, 50, "MIND CORE", 0x00FFFFFF);
        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i)); core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel); } }
        wave += 1;
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_KERNEL

cat << 'INNER_APP' > app/src/main.rs
#![no_std]
#![no_main]

extern crate alloc;
use core::panic::PanicInfo;
use core::arch::asm;
use linked_list_allocator::LockedHeap;
use alloc::format;

#[global_allocator]
static ALLOCATOR: LockedHeap = LockedHeap::empty();
static mut HEAP_MEM: [u8; 256 * 1024] = [0; 256 * 1024];

#[repr(C)]
pub struct SyscallMailbox {
    pub syscall_num: usize,
    pub arg1: usize,
    pub arg2: usize,
    pub result: usize,
}

fn get_os_ticks(mb: *mut SyscallMailbox) -> usize {
    unsafe { (*mb).syscall_num = 1; asm!("int 0x80", options(nostack)); (*mb).result }
}

fn get_os_key(mb: *mut SyscallMailbox) -> u8 {
    unsafe { (*mb).syscall_num = 2; asm!("int 0x80", options(nostack)); (*mb).result as u8 }
}

// Запрос Ядру на создание полноценного системного потока
fn sys_spawn_thread(mb: *mut SyscallMailbox, entry: u64, stack: u64) {
    unsafe {
        (*mb).syscall_num = 3;
        (*mb).arg1 = entry as usize;
        (*mb).arg2 = stack as usize;
        asm!("int 0x80", options(nostack));
    }
}

// Запуск аппаратного вытеснения в Ядре
fn sys_enable_multitasking(mb: *mut SyscallMailbox) {
    unsafe { (*mb).syscall_num = 4; asm!("int 0x80", options(nostack)); }
}

static mut WORK_COUNTER: usize = 0;

#[repr(align(16))]
struct ThreadStack { data: [u64; 1024] }
static mut THREAD_STACK: ThreadStack = ThreadStack { data: [0; 1024] };

// Фоновая задача больше не содержит никаких хаков yield_task!
// Она просто крутит бесконечный цикл. Ядро само отберет у нее процессор на следующей миллисекунде.
fn background_task() {
    loop {
        for _ in 0..500 {
            unsafe { 
                let ptr = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(WORK_COUNTER);
                let count = core::ptr::read_volatile(ptr);
                core::ptr::write_volatile(ptr, count + 1); 
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_char(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, px: usize, py: usize, ascii: u8, color: u32) {
    for row in 0..8 {
        for col in 0..8 {
            let draw_p = match ascii {
                b'A' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0) || row == 3,
                b'B' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && (row == 1 || row == 2 || row == 4 || row == 5 || row == 6)),
                b'C' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0),
                b'D' => col == 0 || (row == 0 || row == 7) && col < 6 || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'E' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7,
                b'F' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3,
                b'G' => (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 7) || ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0) || (row == 4 && col > 3) || (col == 7 && row >= 4),
                b'H' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == 3,
                b'I' => col == 3 || row == 0 || row == 7,
                b'J' => (row == 0 && col > 1) || col == 4 || (row == 7 && col < 4 && col > 0) || (col == 0 && row == 6),
                b'K' => col == 0 || row + col == 4 || row == col + 1,
                b'L' => col == 0 || row == 7,
                b'M' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row == col && col <= 3) || (row + col == 7 && col >= 4),
                b'N' => col == 0 || col == 7 || row == col,
                b'O' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7),
                b'P' => col == 0 || row == 0 || row == 3 || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b'Q' => ((row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7) || ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 6) || (row >= 5 && row == col),
                b'R' => col == 0 || ((row == 0 || row == 3) && col < 6) || (col == 6 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == col && row >= 3),
                b'S' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'T' => row == 0 || col == 3,
                b'U' => (col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 7 || (row == 7 && col > 0 && col < 7),
                b'V' => ((col == 0 || col == 7) && row < 5) || ((col == 1 || col == 6) && row == 5) || ((col == 2 || col == 5) && row == 6) || (col == 3 && row == 7),
                b'W' => col == 0 || col == 7 || (row + col == 7 && col <= 3) || (row == col && col >= 4),
                b'X' => row == col || row + col == 7,
                b'Y' => (row < 4 && (row == col || row + col == 7)) || (row >= 4 && col == 3),
                b'Z' => row == 0 || row == 7 || row + col == 7,
                b'0' => (row == 0 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row > 0 && row < 7,
                b'1' => col == 4 || (row == 1 && col == 3) || row == 7,
                b'2' => (row == 0 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col > 0 && col < 7) || (col == 0 && row > 3 && row < 7) || row == 7,
                b'3' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || col == 7 && row > 0 && row < 7 && row != 3,
                b'4' => col == 6 || (row == 4) || (col == 1 && row < 4) || (row + col == 6 && col < 6),
                b'5' => row == 0 || (col == 0 && row < 3) || (row == 3 && col < 7) || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7) || (row == 7 && col < 7),
                b'6' => (row == 0 && col > 0) || col == 0 || (row == 3 || row == 7) && col < 7 || (col == 7 && row > 3 && row < 7),
                b'7' => row == 0 || col == 7 - row,
                b'8' => (row == 0 || row == 3 || row == 7) && col > 0 && col < 7 || (col == 0 || col == 7) && row != 0 && row != 3 && row != 7,
                b'9' => (row == 7 && col < 7) || col == 7 || (row == 0 || row == 3) && col > 0 || (col == 0 && row > 0 && row < 3),
                b':' => (row == 2 || row == 5) && (col == 3 || col == 4),
                b' ' => false,
                _ => false,
            };
            if draw_p {
                let x = px + col;
                let y = py + row;
                unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb.add(y * stride + x), color); }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn draw_string(bb: *mut u32, stride: usize, start_x: usize, start_y: usize, text: &str, color: u32) {
    let mut cx = start_x;
    for b in text.bytes() {
        let ascii = if b >= b'a' && b <= b'z' { b - 32 } else { b };
        draw_char(bb, stride, cx, start_y, ascii, color);
        cx += 10;
    }
}

#[no_mangle]
#[link_section = ".text._start"]
pub extern "sysv64" fn _start(fb_ptr: *mut u32, bb_ptr: *mut u32, width: usize, height: usize, stride: usize, mb_ptr: *mut SyscallMailbox) -> ! {
    unsafe { ALLOCATOR.lock().init(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(HEAP_MEM) as *mut u8, HEAP_MEM.len()); }
    
    // Регистрируем фоновый поток через ОС
    let stack_top = unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(THREAD_STACK.data) as u64 + (1024 * 8) };
    sys_spawn_thread(mb_ptr, background_task as *const () as u64, stack_top);
    
    // Включаем настоящую многозадачность!
    sys_enable_multitasking(mb_ptr);

    let sin_table: [isize; 36] = [0, 17, 34, 50, 64, 76, 86, 93, 98, 100, 98, 93, 86, 76, 64, 50, 34, 17, 0, -17, -34, -50, -64, -76, -86, -93, -98, -100, -98, -93, -86, -76, -64, -50, -34, -17];
    
    let mut frame_counter: usize = 0; 
    let cx = (width as isize) >> 1;
    let cy = (height as isize) >> 1;
    let total_pixels = height * stride;

    let mut color_theme: u32 = 0x0000FF00;
    let mut last_seen_key: u8 = 0;

    loop {
        // Главный цикл НИКОГДА больше не отдает процессор добровольно.
        // Он занимается только своими делами (графикой).
        
        let key = get_os_key(mb_ptr);
        if key != 0 {
            if key < 0x80 {
                last_seen_key = key;
                if key == 0x39 { color_theme = 0x00FF0000; }
                else if key == 0x1C { color_theme = 0x000000FF; }
                else if key == 0x01 { color_theme = 0x0000FF00; }
            }
        }

        frame_counter += 1;

        for i in 0..total_pixels { unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i), 0x00111111); } }

        let size: isize = 120;
        let mut t_temp = frame_counter / 30; 
        while t_temp >= 36 { t_temp -= 36; }
        let angle = t_temp;
        
        let sin_a = sin_table[angle];
        let mut cos_angle = angle + 9;
        if cos_angle >= 36 { cos_angle -= 36; }
        let cos_a = sin_table[cos_angle];

        for y in (cy - 150)..(cy + 150) {
            for x in (cx - 150)..(cx + 150) {
                if x < 0 || x >= width as isize || y < 0 || y >= height as isize { continue; }
                let dx = x - cx; let dy = y - cy;
                let rx = (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a) / 100;
                let ry = (dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a) / 100;
                
                if rx > -size && rx < size && ry > -size && ry < size {
                    let offset = y as usize * stride + x as usize;
                    unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile(bb_ptr.add(offset), color_theme); }
                }
            }
        }

        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 40, "PREEMPTIVE OS RUNNING", color_theme);

        let f_msg = format!("FRAMES: {}", frame_counter);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 70, &f_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);

        let current_work = unsafe { core::ptr::read_volatile(core::ptr::addr_of!(WORK_COUNTER)) };
        let w_msg = format!("WORK: {}", current_work);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 100, &w_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);

        let os_msg = format!("OS MS: {}", get_os_ticks(mb_ptr));
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 130, &os_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);

        let key_msg = format!("KEY: {}", last_seen_key);
        draw_string(bb_ptr, stride, 40, 160, &key_msg, 0x00FFFFFF);

        for i in 0..total_pixels {
            unsafe {
                let pixel = core::ptr::read_volatile(bb_ptr.add(i));
                core::ptr::write_volatile(fb_ptr.add(i), pixel);
            }
        }
    }
}
#[panic_handler] fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo) -> ! { loop {} }
INNER_APP

cd "$ROOT_DIR/kernel" && cargo build --release
cd "$ROOT_DIR/app" && cargo build --release

OBJCOPY=$(find $(rustc --print sysroot) -name llvm-objcopy | head -n 1)
$OBJCOPY -O binary -j .text -j .rodata -j .data "$ROOT_DIR/kernel/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/kernel" "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/src/kernel.bin"
$OBJCOPY -O binary -j .text -j .rodata -j .data "$ROOT_DIR/app/target/x86_64-unknown-none/release/app" "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/src/app.bin"

cd "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader" && cargo build --release --target x86_64-unknown-uefi
cp "$ROOT_DIR/bootloader/target/x86_64-unknown-uefi/release/bootloader.efi" "$ROOT_DIR/usb_root/EFI/BOOT/BOOTX64.EFI"

echo "Готово! Установлена вытесняющая многозадачность (Preemptive Multitasking)."
